````javascript
import { TrussDatePicker } from '@/components/ui/truss-date-picker';
import ConfirmAlertDialog from '@/components/ConfirmAlertDialog';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from '@/components/ui/table';
const bosForm = {
  arac_id: '',
  bakim_tipi: '',
  tarih: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  km: '',
  maliyet: '',
  notlar: '',
};
export default function Bakimlar() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [araclar, setAraclar] = useState([]);
  const [bakimlar, setBakimlar] = useState([]);
  const [seciliAracId, setSeciliAracId] = useState('tum');
  const [aramaMetni, setAramaMetni] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingBakimId, setEditingBakimId] = useState(null);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState(bosForm);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [deleteBakimId, setDeleteBakimId] = useState(null);
  const verileriYukle = useCallback(async (aracFiltre = seciliAracId) => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const aracRes = await aracService.getAraclar();
      const aracListesi = Array.isArray(aracRes) ? aracRes : aracRes?.araclar || [];
      setAraclar(aracListesi);
      if (aracFiltre && aracFiltre !== 'tum') {
        const bakimRes = await bakimService.getBakimlarByArac(aracFiltre);
        setBakimlar(Array.isArray(bakimRes) ? bakimRes : bakimRes?.bakimlar || []);
      } else {
        const bakimRes = await bakimService.getAllBakimlar();
        setBakimlar(Array.isArray(bakimRes) ? bakimRes : bakimRes?.bakimlar || []);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Bakım kayıtları yüklenemedi.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [seciliAracId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (!token) {
      navigate('/login');
      return;
    }
    verileriYukle(seciliAracId);
  }, [navigate, seciliAracId, verileriYukle]);
  const gosterilenBakimlar = useMemo(() => {
    const q = aramaMetni.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return bakimlar;
    return bakimlar.filter(
      (b) =>
        (b.plaka || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (b.bakim_tipi || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (b.notlar || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [bakimlar, aramaMetni]);
  const toplamMaliyet = useMemo(
    () => gosterilenBakimlar.reduce((sum, b) => sum + (Number(b.maliyet) || 0), 0),
    [gosterilenBakimlar]
  );
  const openAddModal = () => {
    setEditingBakimId(null);
    setFormData({
      ...bosForm,
      arac_id: seciliAracId !== 'tum' ? String(seciliAracId) : araclar[0]?.arac_id ? String(araclar[0].arac_id) : '',
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };
  const openEditModal = (bakim) => {
    setEditingBakimId(bakim.bakim_id);
    setFormData({
      arac_id: String(bakim.arac_id || ''),
      bakim_tipi: bakim.bakim_tipi || '',
      tarih: bakim.tarih ? String(bakim.tarih).slice(0, 10) : '',
      km: bakim.km ?? '',
      maliyet: bakim.maliyet ?? '',
      notlar: bakim.notlar || '',
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };
  const closeModal = () => {
    setIsModalOpen(false);
    setEditingBakimId(null);
    setFormData(bosForm);
  };
  const handleInputChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
  };
  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formData.arac_id || !formData.bakim_tipi || !formData.tarih || formData.km === '' || formData.maliyet === '') {
      toast.error('Lütfen zorunlu alanları doldurun.');
      return;
    }
    const payload = {
      arac_id: Number(formData.arac_id),
      bakim_tipi: formData.bakim_tipi.trim(),
      tarih: formData.tarih,
      km: Number(formData.km),
      maliyet: Number(formData.maliyet),
      notlar: formData.notlar.trim() || null,
    };
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (editingBakimId) {
        await bakimService.bakimGuncelle(editingBakimId, payload);
        toast.success('Bakım kaydı güncellendi.');
      } else {
        await bakimService.bakimEkle(payload);
        toast.success('Bakım kaydı eklendi.');
      }
      closeModal();
      await verileriYukle(seciliAracId);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Bakım kaydedilemedi.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };
  const handleDelete = async () => {
    try {
      await bakimService.bakimSil(deleteBakimId);
      setBakimlar((prev) => prev.filter((b) => b.bakim_id !== deleteBakimId));
      toast.success('Bakım kaydı silindi.');
      setDeleteBakimId(null);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Bakım kaydı silinemedi.');
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Kompakt Filtre ve Aksiyon Çubuğu (Toolbar) */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Car className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <select
            value={seciliAracId}
            onChange={(e) => setSeciliAracId(e.target.value)}
            className="h-8 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2.5 text-xs text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring"
          >
            <option value="tum">Tüm Araçlar</option>
            {araclar.map((a) => (
              <option key={a.arac_id} value={a.arac_id}>
                {a.plaka} — {a.marka} {a.model}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-60">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-2 size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
            <Input
              type="search"
              placeholder="Plaka, bakım tipi veya not ara…"
              value={aramaMetni}
              onChange={(e) => setAramaMetni(e.target.value)}
              className="h-8 pl-8 text-xs"
            />
          </div>
          <Button size="sm" className="h-8 gap-1.5 text-xs" onClick={openAddModal}>
            <Plus className="size-3.5" /> Yeni Bakım Ekle
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Kompakt Tek Satırlık KPI Şeridi */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Wrench className="size-4 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            Toplam Bakım Kaydı
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">{gosterilenBakimlar.length}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Banknote className="size-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
            Toplam Bakım Gideri
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">
            {toplamMaliyet.toLocaleString('tr-TR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} ₺
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Car className="size-4 shrink-0 text-sky-500" />
            Kayıtlı Araç Sayısı
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">{araclar.length}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Bakım Tablosu */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted/40" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : gosterilenBakimlar.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-border py-16 text-center text-muted-foreground">
          <Wrench className="mx-auto mb-3 size-8 opacity-40" />
          <p className="text-sm font-medium">Henüz bakım kaydı bulunmuyor.</p>
          <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="mt-3 gap-1.5" onClick={openAddModal}>
            <Plus className="size-4" /> İlk Bakımı Ekle
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border">
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow className="bg-muted/30">
                <TableHead>Plaka / Araç</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Bakım Tipi</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Tarih</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Kilometre</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Maliyet</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Notlar</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="text-right">İşlemler</TableHead>
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {gosterilenBakimlar.map((bakim) => (
                <TableRow key={bakim.bakim_id} className="hover:bg-muted/30">
                  <TableCell className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold">
                    <div>{bakim.plaka || `#${bakim.arac_id}`}</div>
                    {(bakim.marka || bakim.model) && (
                      <div className="font-sans text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                        {bakim.marka} {bakim.model}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1">
                      <Wrench className="size-3" />
                      {bakim.bakim_tipi}
                    </Badge>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-sm">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Calendar className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                      {bakim.tarih ? new Date(bakim.tarih).toLocaleDateString('tr-TR') : '-'}
                    </span>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-sm">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Gauge className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                      {Number(bakim.km || 0).toLocaleString('tr-TR')} KM
                    </span>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-sm font-semibold">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Banknote className="size-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
                      {Number(bakim.maliyet || 0).toLocaleString('tr-TR', {
                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                      })}{' '}
                      ₺
                    </span>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {bakim.notlar || '-'}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-right">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                      <Button
                        size="icon"
                        variant="ghost"
                        className="size-7"
                        onClick={() => openEditModal(bakim)}
                      >
                        <Pencil className="size-3.5" />
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        size="icon"
                        variant="ghost"
                        className="size-7 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                        onClick={() => setDeleteBakimId(bakim.bakim_id)}
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
        </div>
      )}
      <ConfirmAlertDialog
        open={deleteBakimId !== null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setDeleteBakimId(null)}
        title="Bakım kaydını sil?"
        description="Bu bakım kaydı kalıcı olarak silinecek."
        onConfirm={handleDelete}
      />
      {/* Ekle / Düzenle Modalı */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 px-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white/95 p-6 text-slate-900 dark:border-white/10 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 dark:text-zinc-100 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pb-3">
              <h3 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                <Wrench className="size-4 text-primary" />
                {editingBakimId ? 'Bakım Kaydını Düzenle' : 'Yeni Bakım Ekle'}
              </h3>
              <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" className="size-7 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-white" onClick={closeModal}>
                <X className="size-4" />
              </Button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Araç</Label>
                <select
                  name="arac_id"
                  value={formData.arac_id}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  required
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                >
                  <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">Araç seçin…</option>
                  {araclar.map((a) => (
                    <option key={a.arac_id} value={a.arac_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                      {a.plaka} — {a.marka} {a.model}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Bakım Tipi</Label>
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  name="bakim_tipi"
                  value={formData.bakim_tipi}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  placeholder="Örn: Periyodik Bakım, Fren Balatası Değişimi"
                  className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Tarih</Label>
                  <TrussDatePicker
                    name="tarih"
                    value={formData.tarih}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    placeholder="Bakım tarihi seçin"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Kilometre (KM)</Label>
                  <Input
                    type="number"
                    name="km"
                    min="0"
                    value={formData.km}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    placeholder="125000"
                    className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Maliyet (₺)</Label>
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.01"
                  min="0"
                  name="maliyet"
                  value={formData.maliyet}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  placeholder="4500.00"
                  className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  <FileText className="size-3.5" /> Notlar (İsteğe Bağlı)
                </Label>
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  name="notlar"
                  value={formData.notlar}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  placeholder="Servis adı, değişen parçalar vb."
                  className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pt-3">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={closeModal}
                  className="border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-700/80 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:border-primary/50 dark:hover:bg-primary/15 dark:hover:text-white"
                >
                  İptal
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting}>
                  {isSubmitting
                    ? 'Kaydediliyor…'
                    : editingBakimId
                      ? 'Güncelle'
                      : 'Kaydet'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/pages/EkipYonetimi.jsx
````javascript
import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  Users,
  UserPlus,
  Car,
  ShieldCheck,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  Link2,
  X,
  CheckCircle2,
  Search,
} from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import adminService from '@/services/adminService';
import aracService from '@/services/aracService';
const ROLE_BADGES = {
  SYSTEM_ADMIN: {
    label: 'Sistem Admini',
    className: 'bg-purple-500/15 text-purple-400 border-purple-500/30',
  },
  FLEET_MANAGER: {
    label: 'Filo Yöneticisi',
    className: 'bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30',
  },
  DRIVER: {
    label: 'Şoför',
    className: 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/30',
  },
};
const EkipYonetimi = () => {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [vehicles, setVehicles] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  // Modals
  const [isAddDriverOpen, setIsAddDriverOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isAssignOpen, setIsAssignOpen] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  // Forms
  const [driverForm, setDriverForm] = useState({
    ad_soyad: '',
    email: '',
    sifre: '',
    telefon: '',
  });
  const [assignForm, setAssignForm] = useState({
    sofor_user_id: '',
    arac_id: '',
  });
  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [userList, aracRes] = await Promise.all([
        adminService.getUsers(),
        aracService.getAraclar(),
      ]);
      setUsers(Array.isArray(userList) ? userList : []);
      const aracList = Array.isArray(aracRes) ? aracRes : aracRes?.araclar || [];
      setVehicles(aracList);
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error?.response?.data?.message || 'Ekip verileri yüklenemedi.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData();
  }, [fetchData]);
  const handleCreateDriver = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!driverForm.ad_soyad || !driverForm.email || !driverForm.sifre) {
      toast.error('Lütfen zorunlu alanları doldurun.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await adminService.createDriver(driverForm);
      toast.success('Yeni şoför filoya eklendi.');
      setDriverForm({ ad_soyad: '', email: '', sifre: '', telefon: '' });
      setIsAddDriverOpen(false);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error?.response?.data?.message || 'Şoför eklenemedi.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };
  const handleAssignVehicle = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!assignForm.sofor_user_id || !assignForm.arac_id) {
      toast.error('Lütfen şoför ve araç seçin.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await adminService.assignVehicle({
        sofor_user_id: Number(assignForm.sofor_user_id),
        arac_id: Number(assignForm.arac_id),
      });
      toast.success('Araç şoföre başarıyla zimmetlendi.');
      setAssignForm({ sofor_user_id: '', arac_id: '' });
      setIsAssignOpen(false);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error?.response?.data?.message || 'Araç zimmetlenemedi.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };
  const openAssignModalForDriver = (driverId = '') => {
    setAssignForm({
      sofor_user_id: driverId ? String(driverId) : '',
      arac_id: vehicles[0]?.arac_id ? String(vehicles[0].arac_id) : '',
    });
    setIsAssignOpen(true);
  };
  const driversOnly = users.filter((u) => u.role === 'DRIVER');
  const assignedCount = users.reduce(
    (sum, u) => sum + (Array.isArray(u.zimmetli_araclar) ? u.zimmetli_araclar.length : 0),
    0
  );
  const filteredUsers = users.filter((u) => {
    const q = searchTerm.toLowerCase();
    return (
      u.ad_soyad?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      u.email?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      u.role?.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  });
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Kompakt Filtre ve Aksiyon Çubuğu (Toolbar) */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div className="relative w-full sm:w-64">
          <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 size-3.5 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ad, e-posta veya rol ara..."
            className="h-8 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background pl-8 pr-3 text-xs text-foreground placeholder:text-muted-foreground focus:border-primary focus:outline-none"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => openAssignModalForDriver('')}
            className="inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card px-3 text-xs font-medium text-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-accent transition-colors"
          >
            <Link2 className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
            Araç Zimmetle
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsAddDriverOpen(true)}
            className="inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-primary px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-primary/90 transition-colors"
          >
            <UserPlus className="size-3.5" />
            Yeni Şoför Ekle
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Kompakt Tek Satırlık KPI Şeridi */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Users className="size-4 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            Toplam Ekip Üyesi
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums text-foreground">{users.length}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <ShieldCheck className="size-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
            Aktif Şoför Sayısı
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums text-foreground">{driversOnly.length}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Car className="size-4 shrink-0 text-blue-400" />
            Zimmetli Araçlar
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums text-foreground">{assignedCount}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Ekip Tablosu */}
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="border-b border-border bg-muted/40 text-xs uppercase text-muted-foreground">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-3 py-2.5 font-medium">Ad Soyad</th>
                <th className="px-3 py-2.5 font-medium">İletişim</th>
                <th className="px-3 py-2.5 font-medium">Rol</th>
                <th className="px-3 py-2.5 font-medium">Zimmetli Araçlar</th>
                <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right font-medium">İşlem</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-border">
              {loading ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={5} className="px-3 py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">
                    Ekip listesi yükleniyor...
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : filteredUsers.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={5} className="px-3 py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">
                    Kayıtlı ekip üyesi bulunamadı.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                filteredUsers.map((member) => {
                  const badge = ROLE_BADGES[member.role] || ROLE_BADGES.DRIVER;
                  const zimmetler = Array.isArray(member.zimmetli_araclar)
                    ? member.zimmetli_araclar
                    : [];
                  return (
                    <tr key={member.user_id} className="hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors">
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-foreground">
                        {member.ad_soyad}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-muted-foreground">
                        <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 text-xs">
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <Mail className="size-3.5" />
                            {member.email}
                          </span>
                          {member.telefon && (
                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                              <Phone className="size-3.5" />
                              {member.telefon}
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                        <span
                          className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${badge.className}`}
                        >
                          {badge.label}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                        {zimmetler.length > 0 ? (
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                            {zimmetler.map((z) => (
                              <span
                                key={z.atama_id || z.arac_id}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-accent px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-foreground"
                              >
                                <Car className="size-3 text-primary" />
                                {z.plaka} ({z.marka} {z.model})
                              </span>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Zimmetli araç yok</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => openAssignModalForDriver(member.user_id)}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-foreground hover:bg-accent transition-colors"
                        >
                          <Car className="size-3.5 text-primary" />
                          Araç Zimmetle
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* MODAL 1: Yeni Şoför Ekle */}
      {isAddDriverOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white/95 p-6 text-slate-900 dark:border-white/10 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 dark:text-zinc-100 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pb-3">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                <UserPlus className="size-5 text-primary" />
                Yeni Şoför Ekle (DRIVER)
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsAddDriverOpen(false)}
                className="rounded-lg p-1 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-white"
              >
                <X className="size-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleCreateDriver} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1.5">
                  Ad Soyad *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={driverForm.ad_soyad}
                  onChange={(e) => setDriverForm({ ...driverForm, ad_soyad: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="Örn: Ahmet Yılmaz"
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1.5">
                  E-Posta Adresi *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={driverForm.email}
                  onChange={(e) => setDriverForm({ ...driverForm, email: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="sofor@sirket.com"
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1.5">
                  Geçici Şifre *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  minLength={6}
                  value={driverForm.sifre}
                  onChange={(e) => setDriverForm({ ...driverForm, sifre: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="En az 6 karakter"
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1.5">
                  Telefon (Opsiyonel)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={driverForm.telefon}
                  onChange={(e) => setDriverForm({ ...driverForm, telefon: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="05XX XXX XX XX"
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pt-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsAddDriverOpen(false)}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-700/80 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:border-primary/50 dark:hover:bg-primary/15 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
                >
                  İptal
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-md shadow-primary/20 hover:bg-primary/90 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" />
                  {submitting ? 'Kaydediliyor...' : 'Şoförü Kaydet'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {/* MODAL 2: Araç Zimmetle */}
      {isAssignOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white/95 p-6 text-slate-900 dark:border-white/10 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 dark:text-zinc-100 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pb-3">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Car className="size-5 text-primary" />
                Personele Araç Zimmetle
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsAssignOpen(false)}
                className="rounded-lg p-1 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-white"
              >
                <X className="size-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleAssignVehicle} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1.5">
                  Şoför / Ekip Üyesi Seçin *
                </label>
                <select
                  required
                  value={assignForm.sofor_user_id}
                  onChange={(e) => setAssignForm({ ...assignForm, sofor_user_id: e.target.value })}
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                >
                  <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">-- Kullanıcı Seçin --</option>
                  {users.map((u) => (
                    <option key={u.user_id} value={u.user_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                      {u.ad_soyad} ({u.role})
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1.5">
                  Zimmetlenecek Araç *
                </label>
                <select
                  required
                  value={assignForm.arac_id}
                  onChange={(e) => setAssignForm({ ...assignForm, arac_id: e.target.value })}
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                >
                  <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">-- Araç Seçin --</option>
                  {vehicles.map((v) => (
                    <option key={v.arac_id} value={v.arac_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                      {v.plaka} — {v.marka} {v.model} ({v.yil})
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pt-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsAssignOpen(false)}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-700/80 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:border-primary/50 dark:hover:bg-primary/15 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
                >
                  İptal
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-md shadow-primary/20 hover:bg-primary/90 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <Link2 className="size-4" />
                  {submitting ? 'Atanıyor...' : 'Zimmeti Tamamla'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
export default EkipYonetimi;
````

## File: Frontend/src/pages/ParcaTakip.jsx
````javascript
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import {
  Cog,
  Plus,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  Calendar,
  Gauge,
  Car,
  Search,
  X,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
} from 'lucide-react';
import parcaService from '@/services/parcaService';
import aracService from '@/services/aracService';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { TrussDatePicker } from '@/components/ui/truss-date-picker';
import ConfirmAlertDialog from '@/components/ConfirmAlertDialog';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from '@/components/ui/table';
const bosForm = {
  arac_id: '',
  parca_adi: '',
  son_degisim_km: '',
  degisim_araligi_km: '',
  son_degisim_tarihi: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
};
function hesaplaParcaDurumu(parca) {
  const guncelKm = Number(parca.guncel_km) || 0;
  const sonKm = Number(parca.son_degisim_km) || 0;
  const aralikKm =
    parca.degisim_araligi_km !== null && parca.degisim_araligi_km !== undefined
      ? Number(parca.degisim_araligi_km)
      : null;
  if (!aralikKm || aralikKm <= 0) {
    return {
      kalanKm: null,
      yuzde: null,
      durum: 'tarih_bazli',
      etiket: 'Tarih Bazlı',
    };
  }
  const kullanilanKm = Math.max(0, guncelKm - sonKm);
  const kalanKm = aralikKm - kullanilanKm;
  const yuzde = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((kalanKm / aralikKm) * 100)));
  if (kalanKm <= 0) {
    return { kalanKm, yuzde: 0, durum: 'kritik', etiket: 'Değişim Gerekli' };
  }
  if (kalanKm <= 1500) {
    return { kalanKm, yuzde, durum: 'uyari', etiket: 'Yaklaşıyor' };
  }
  return { kalanKm, yuzde, durum: 'iyi', etiket: 'İyi Durumda' };
}
export default function ParcaTakip() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [araclar, setAraclar] = useState([]);
  const [parcalar, setParcalar] = useState([]);
  const [seciliAracId, setSeciliAracId] = useState('tum');
  const [aramaMetni, setAramaMetni] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deleteParcaId, setDeleteParcaId] = useState(null);
  const [editingParcaId, setEditingParcaId] = useState(null);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState(bosForm);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const verileriYukle = useCallback(
    async (aracFiltre = seciliAracId) => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const aracRes = await aracService.getAraclar();
        const aracListesi = Array.isArray(aracRes) ? aracRes : aracRes?.araclar || [];
        setAraclar(aracListesi);
        if (aracFiltre && aracFiltre !== 'tum') {
          const parcaRes = await parcaService.getParcalarByArac(aracFiltre);
          setParcalar(Array.isArray(parcaRes) ? parcaRes : parcaRes?.parcalar || []);
        } else {
          const parcaRes = await parcaService.getAllParcalar();
          setParcalar(Array.isArray(parcaRes) ? parcaRes : parcaRes?.parcalar || []);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Parça kayıtları yüklenemedi.');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [seciliAracId]
  );
  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (!token) {
      navigate('/login');
      return;
    }
    verileriYukle(seciliAracId);
  }, [navigate, seciliAracId, verileriYukle]);
  const gosterilenParcalar = useMemo(() => {
    const q = aramaMetni.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return parcalar;
    return parcalar.filter(
      (p) =>
        (p.plaka || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (p.parca_adi || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (p.marka || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (p.model || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [parcalar, aramaMetni]);
  const ozet = useMemo(() => {
    let kritik = 0;
    let uyari = 0;
    gosterilenParcalar.forEach((p) => {
      const d = hesaplaParcaDurumu(p).durum;
      if (d === 'kritik') kritik += 1;
      else if (d === 'uyari') uyari += 1;
    });
    return { toplam: gosterilenParcalar.length, kritik, uyari };
  }, [gosterilenParcalar]);
  const openAddModal = () => {
    setEditingParcaId(null);
    setFormData({
      ...bosForm,
      arac_id:
        seciliAracId !== 'tum'
          ? String(seciliAracId)
          : araclar[0]?.arac_id
            ? String(araclar[0].arac_id)
            : '',
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };
  const openEditModal = (parca) => {
    setEditingParcaId(parca.parca_id);
    setFormData({
      arac_id: String(parca.arac_id || ''),
      parca_adi: parca.parca_adi || '',
      son_degisim_km: parca.son_degisim_km ?? 0,
      degisim_araligi_km: parca.degisim_araligi_km ?? '',
      son_degisim_tarihi: parca.son_degisim_tarihi
        ? String(parca.son_degisim_tarihi).slice(0, 10)
        : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };
  const closeModal = () => {
    setIsModalOpen(false);
    setEditingParcaId(null);
    setFormData(bosForm);
  };
  const handleInputChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
  };
  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (
      !formData.arac_id ||
      !formData.parca_adi.trim() ||
      formData.son_degisim_km === '' ||
      !formData.son_degisim_tarihi
    ) {
      toast.error('Lütfen zorunlu alanları doldurun.');
      return;
    }
    const payload = {
      arac_id: Number(formData.arac_id),
      parca_adi: formData.parca_adi.trim(),
      son_degisim_km: Number(formData.son_degisim_km),
      degisim_araligi_km:
        formData.degisim_araligi_km === '' || formData.degisim_araligi_km === null
          ? null
          : Number(formData.degisim_araligi_km),
      son_degisim_tarihi: formData.son_degisim_tarihi,
    };
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (editingParcaId) {
        await parcaService.parcaGuncelle(editingParcaId, payload);
        toast.success('Parça kaydı güncellendi.');
      } else {
        await parcaService.parcaEkle(payload);
        toast.success('Parça takibe alındı.');
      }
      closeModal();
      await verileriYukle(seciliAracId);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Parça kaydedilemedi.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };
  const handleDelete = async () => {
    try {
      await parcaService.parcaSil(deleteParcaId);
      setParcalar((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.parca_id !== deleteParcaId));
      toast.success('Parça kaydı silindi.');
      setDeleteParcaId(null);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Parça silinemedi.');
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Kompakt Filtre ve Aksiyon Çubuğu (Toolbar) */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Car className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <select
            value={seciliAracId}
            onChange={(e) => setSeciliAracId(e.target.value)}
            className="h-8 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2.5 text-xs text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring"
          >
            <option value="tum">Tüm Araçlar</option>
            {araclar.map((a) => (
              <option key={a.arac_id} value={a.arac_id}>
                {a.plaka} — {a.marka} {a.model}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-60">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-2 size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
            <Input
              type="search"
              placeholder="Plaka veya parça adı ara…"
              value={aramaMetni}
              onChange={(e) => setAramaMetni(e.target.value)}
              className="h-8 pl-8 text-xs"
            />
          </div>
          <Button size="sm" className="h-8 gap-1.5 text-xs" onClick={openAddModal}>
            <Plus className="size-3.5" /> Yeni Parça Ekle
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Kompakt Tek Satırlık KPI Şeridi */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Cog className="size-4 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            Takip Edilen Parça
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">{ozet.toplam}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Clock className="size-4 shrink-0 text-amber-500" />
            Değişimi Yaklaşan
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight text-amber-500">{ozet.uyari}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <AlertTriangle className="size-4 shrink-0 text-destructive" />
            Ömrü Dolan / Kritik
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight text-destructive">{ozet.kritik}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Tablo */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted/40" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : gosterilenParcalar.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-border py-16 text-center text-muted-foreground">
          <Cog className="mx-auto mb-3 size-8 opacity-40" />
          <p className="text-sm font-medium">Takip edilen parça kaydı bulunamadı.</p>
          <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="mt-3 gap-1.5" onClick={openAddModal}>
            <Plus className="size-4" /> Yeni Parça Ekle
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border">
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow className="bg-muted/30">
                <TableHead>Plaka / Araç</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Parça Adı</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Son Değişim Tarihi</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Son Değişim KM</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Değişim Aralığı</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Kalan Ömür</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Durum</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="text-right">İşlemler</TableHead>
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {gosterilenParcalar.map((parca) => {
                const analiz = hesaplaParcaDurumu(parca);
                return (
                  <TableRow key={parca.parca_id} className="hover:bg-muted/30">
                    <TableCell className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold">
                      <div>{parca.plaka || `#${parca.arac_id}`}</div>
                      {(parca.marka || parca.model) && (
                        <div className="font-sans text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                          {parca.marka} {parca.model}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="font-medium">{parca.parca_adi}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-sm">
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Calendar className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                        {parca.son_degisim_tarihi
                          ? new Date(parca.son_degisim_tarihi).toLocaleDateString('tr-TR')
                          : '-'}
                      </span>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-sm">
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Gauge className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                        {Number(parca.son_degisim_km || 0).toLocaleString('tr-TR')} KM
                      </span>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      {parca.degisim_araligi_km
                        ? `${Number(parca.degisim_araligi_km).toLocaleString('tr-TR')} KM`
                        : 'Tarih Bazlı'}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      {analiz.kalanKm === null ? (
                        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">—</span>
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-36 space-y-1">
                          <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
                            <span className="font-medium">
                              {analiz.kalanKm <= 0
                                ? `${Math.abs(analiz.kalanKm).toLocaleString('tr-TR')} KM aşım`
                                : `${analiz.kalanKm.toLocaleString('tr-TR')} KM`}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-muted-foreground">%{analiz.yuzde}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-muted">
                            <div
                              className={`h-full transition-all ${
                                analiz.durum === 'kritik'
                                  ? 'bg-destructive'
                                  : analiz.durum === 'uyari'
                                    ? 'bg-amber-500'
                                    : 'bg-emerald-500'
                              }`}
                              style={{ width: `${analiz.yuzde}%` }}
                            />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      {analiz.durum === 'kritik' && (
                        <Badge variant="destructive" className="gap-1">
                          <AlertTriangle className="size-3" /> {analiz.etiket}
                        </Badge>
                      )}
                      {analiz.durum === 'uyari' && (
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1 border-amber-500/50 text-amber-500">
                          <Clock className="size-3" /> {analiz.etiket}
                        </Badge>
                      )}
                      {analiz.durum === 'iyi' && (
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1 border-emerald-500/50 text-emerald-500">
                          <CheckCircle2 className="size-3" /> {analiz.etiket}
                        </Badge>
                      )}
                      {analiz.durum === 'tarih_bazli' && (
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="text-muted-foreground">
                          {analiz.etiket}
                        </Badge>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                        <Button
                          size="icon"
                          variant="ghost"
                          className="size-7"
                          onClick={() => openEditModal(parca)}
                        >
                          <Pencil className="size-3.5" />
                        </Button>
                        <Button
                          size="icon"
                          variant="ghost"
                          className="size-7 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                          onClick={() => setDeleteParcaId(parca.parca_id)}
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                );
              })}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
        </div>
      )}
      <ConfirmAlertDialog
        open={deleteParcaId !== null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setDeleteParcaId(null)}
        title="Parça kaydını sil?"
        description="Bu parça takip kaydı kalıcı olarak silinecek."
        onConfirm={handleDelete}
      />
      {/* Ekle / Düzenle Modalı */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 px-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white/95 p-6 text-slate-900 dark:border-white/10 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 dark:text-zinc-100 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pb-3">
              <h3 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                <Cog className="size-4 text-primary" />
                {editingParcaId ? 'Parça Kaydını Düzenle' : 'Yeni Parça Ekle'}
              </h3>
              <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" className="size-7 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-white" onClick={closeModal}>
                <X className="size-4" />
              </Button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Araç</Label>
                <select
                  name="arac_id"
                  value={formData.arac_id}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  disabled={Boolean(editingParcaId)}
                  required
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25 disabled:opacity-60"
                >
                  <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">Araç seçin…</option>
                  {araclar.map((a) => (
                    <option key={a.arac_id} value={a.arac_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                      {a.plaka} — {a.marka} {a.model}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Parça Adı</Label>
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  name="parca_adi"
                  value={formData.parca_adi}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  placeholder="Örn: Motor Yağı, Triger Kayışı, Fren Balataları"
                  className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Son Değişim KM</Label>
                  <Input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    name="son_degisim_km"
                    value={formData.son_degisim_km}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    placeholder="120000"
                    className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Değişim Aralığı (KM)</Label>
                  <Input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    name="degisim_araligi_km"
                    value={formData.degisim_araligi_km}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    placeholder="Örn: 10000 (Boş = Tarih bazlı)"
                    className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Son Değişim Tarihi</Label>
                <TrussDatePicker
                  name="son_degisim_tarihi"
                  value={formData.son_degisim_tarihi}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  placeholder="Son değişim tarihi seçin"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pt-3">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={closeModal}
                  className="border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-700/80 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:border-primary/50 dark:hover:bg-primary/15 dark:hover:text-white"
                >
                  İptal
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting}>
                  {isSubmitting
                    ? 'Kaydediliyor…'
                    : editingParcaId
                      ? 'Güncelle'
                      : 'Kaydet'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/pages/Raporlar.jsx
````javascript
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import {
  BarChart3,
  Printer,
  Banknote,
  Fuel,
  Wrench,
  Truck,
  AlertTriangle,
  PieChart as PieChartIcon,
  TrendingUp,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  AreaChart,
  Area,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
  PieChart,
  Pie,
  Cell,
} from 'recharts';
import raporService from '@/services/raporService';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from '@/components/ui/table';
const PIE_COLORS = ['#0ea5e9', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#8b5cf6', '#ef4444', '#ec4899'];
const paraFormatla = (deger) =>
  `${Number(deger || 0).toLocaleString('tr-TR', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })} ₺`;
const ayEtiketiFormatla = (ayStr) => {
  if (!ayStr) return '';
  const [yil, ay] = String(ayStr).split('-');
  const aylar = ['Oca', 'Şub', 'Mar', 'Nis', 'May', 'Haz', 'Tem', 'Ağu', 'Eyl', 'Eki', 'Kas', 'Ara'];
  const idx = parseInt(ay, 10) - 1;
  return `${aylar[idx] || ay} ${yil}`;
};
export default function Raporlar() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [genelOzet, setGenelOzet] = useState({
    toplam_arac: 0,
    toplam_yakit_gideri: 0,
    toplam_bakim_gideri: 0,
    toplam_gider: 0,
    yaklasan_parca_sayisi: 0,
  });
  const [aylikTrend, setAylikTrend] = useState([]);
  const [aracDagilimi, setAracDagilimi] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const verileriYukle = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [ozetRes, trendRes, dagilimRes] = await Promise.all([
        raporService.getGenelOzet(),
        raporService.getAylikHarcamaTrendi(),
        raporService.getAracMaliyetDagilimi(),
      ]);
      if (ozetRes) setGenelOzet(ozetRes);
      setAylikTrend(Array.isArray(trendRes) ? trendRes : []);
      setAracDagilimi(Array.isArray(dagilimRes) ? dagilimRes : []);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Rapor verileri yüklenemedi.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (!token) {
      navigate('/login');
      return;
    }
    verileriYukle();
  }, [navigate, verileriYukle]);
  const grafikTrendVerisi = useMemo(
    () =>
      aylikTrend.map((item) => ({
        ay: ayEtiketiFormatla(item.ay),
        Yakit: Number(item.toplam_yakit) || 0,
        Bakim: Number(item.toplam_bakim) || 0,
        Toplam: Number(item.toplam_harcama) || 0,
      })),
    [aylikTrend]
  );
  const pastaVerisi = useMemo(() => {
    const veriler = aracDagilimi
      .map((a) => ({
        name: a.plaka || `#${a.arac_id}`,
        value: Number(a.toplam_maliyet) || 0,
      }))
      .filter((item) => item.value > 0);
    if (veriler.length > 0) return veriler;
    const yakit = Number(genelOzet.toplam_yakit_gideri) || 0;
    const bakim = Number(genelOzet.toplam_bakim_gideri) || 0;
    if (yakit === 0 && bakim === 0) return [];
    return [
      { name: 'Yakıt Gideri', value: yakit },
      { name: 'Bakım Gideri', value: bakim },
    ];
  }, [aracDagilimi, genelOzet]);
  const handlePrint = () => {
    window.print();
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 print:space-y-4">
      {/* Kompakt Aksiyon Çubuğu (Toolbar) */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-end">
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          className="h-8 gap-1.5 text-xs print:hidden"
          onClick={handlePrint}
        >
          <Printer className="size-3.5" /> Raporu Yazdır (Print)
        </Button>
      </div>
      {/* Kompakt Tek Satırlık KPI Şeridi */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Banknote className="size-4 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            Toplam Filo Gideri
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">
            {paraFormatla(genelOzet.toplam_gider)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Fuel className="size-4 shrink-0 text-sky-500" />
            Yakıt Gideri
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight text-sky-500">
            {paraFormatla(genelOzet.toplam_yakit_gideri)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Wrench className="size-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
            Bakım Gideri
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight text-emerald-500">
            {paraFormatla(genelOzet.toplam_bakim_gideri)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Truck className="size-4 shrink-0 text-amber-500" />
            Aktif Araç & Uyarı
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">
              {genelOzet.toplam_arac}
            </span>
            {Number(genelOzet.yaklasan_parca_sayisi) > 0 && (
              <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1 border-amber-500/50 px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] tabular-nums text-amber-500">
                <AlertTriangle className="size-3" />
                {genelOzet.yaklasan_parca_sayisi}
              </Badge>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Orta Bölüm: Grafikler */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/40 lg:col-span-2" />
          <div className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/40" />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {/* Sol: Son 6 Ay Harcama Trendi (AreaChart) */}
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5 shadow-sm lg:col-span-2">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base font-semibold">
                  <TrendingUp className="size-4 text-primary" />
                  Aylık Harcama Trendi (Son 6 Ay)
                </h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  Ay bazında yakıt ve bakım giderlerinin karşılaştırmalı analizi
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="h-72 w-full">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <AreaChart data={grafikTrendVerisi} margin={{ top: 10, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 0 }}>
                  <defs>
                    <linearGradient id="colorYakit" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                      <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#0ea5e9" stopOpacity={0.35} />
                      <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#0ea5e9" stopOpacity={0} />
                    </linearGradient>
                    <linearGradient id="colorBakim" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                      <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#10b981" stopOpacity={0.35} />
                      <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#10b981" stopOpacity={0} />
                    </linearGradient>
                  </defs>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" strokeOpacity={0.15} />
                  <XAxis dataKey="ay" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <Tooltip
                    formatter={(value) => paraFormatla(value)}
                    contentStyle={{
                      backgroundColor: 'hsl(var(--card))',
                      borderColor: 'hsl(var(--border))',
                      borderRadius: '0.75rem',
                    }}
                  />
                  <Legend />
                  <Area
                    type="monotone"
                    dataKey="Yakit"
                    name="Yakıt Gideri"
                    stroke="#0ea5e9"
                    strokeWidth={2}
                    fillOpacity={1}
                    fill="url(#colorYakit)"
                  />
                  <Area
                    type="monotone"
                    dataKey="Bakim"
                    name="Bakım Gideri"
                    stroke="#10b981"
                    strokeWidth={2}
                    fillOpacity={1}
                    fill="url(#colorBakim)"
                  />
                </AreaChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </div>
          </div>
          {/* Sağ: Maliyet Dağılımı (Donut / PieChart) */}
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5 shadow-sm">
            <div className="mb-4">
              <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base font-semibold">
                <PieChartIcon className="size-4 text-primary" />
                Maliyet Dağılımı
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                Araç bazında toplam harcama payları
              </p>
            </div>
            {pastaVerisi.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex h-72 items-center justify-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Henüz maliyet verisi bulunmuyor.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="h-72 w-full">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie
                      data={pastaVerisi}
                      cx="50%"
                      cy="45%"
                      innerRadius={55}
                      outerRadius={85}
                      paddingAngle={4}
                      dataKey="value"
                    >
                      {pastaVerisi.map((entry, index) => (
                        <Cell
                          key={`cell-${entry.name}`}
                          fill={PIE_COLORS[index % PIE_COLORS.length]}
                        />
                      ))}
                    </Pie>
                    <Tooltip formatter={(value) => paraFormatla(value)} />
                    <Legend verticalAlign="bottom" height={36} />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {/* Alt Bölüm: Araç Bazlı Maliyet Tablosu */}
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-card shadow-sm">
        <div className="border-b border-border px-5 py-4">
          <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">Araç Bazlı Maliyet Özeti</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            Her aracın yakıt, bakım ve toplam operasyonel gider kırılımı
          </p>
        </div>
        {aracDagilimi.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Kayıtlı araç bulunamadı.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow className="bg-muted/30">
                <TableHead>Plaka</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Marka / Model</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="text-right">Yakıt Gideri</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="text-right">Bakım Gideri</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="text-right">Toplam Maliyet</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="text-right">Filo Payı</TableHead>
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {aracDagilimi.map((arac) => {
                const toplamFilo = Number(genelOzet.toplam_gider) || 0;
                const aracMaliyet = Number(arac.toplam_maliyet) || 0;
                const pay = toplamFilo > 0 ? ((aracMaliyet / toplamFilo) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                return (
                  <TableRow key={arac.arac_id} className="hover:bg-muted/30">
                    <TableCell className="font-mono font-semibold">{arac.plaka}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      {arac.marka} {arac.model} {arac.yil ? `(${arac.yil})` : ''}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right text-sm text-sky-500">
                      {paraFormatla(arac.toplam_yakit)}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right text-sm text-emerald-500">
                      {paraFormatla(arac.toplam_bakim)}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right text-sm font-bold">
                      {paraFormatla(arac.toplam_maliyet)}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right">
                      <Badge variant="outline">%{pay}</Badge>
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                );
              })}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/pages/Yakitlar.jsx
````javascript
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import {
  Fuel,
  Plus,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  Calendar,
  Gauge,
  Banknote,
  Droplets,
  Car,
  Search,
  X,
  FileText,
} from 'lucide-react';
import yakitService from '@/services/yakitService';
import aracService from '@/services/aracService';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { TrussDatePicker } from '@/components/ui/truss-date-picker';
import ConfirmAlertDialog from '@/components/ConfirmAlertDialog';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from '@/components/ui/table';
const YAKIT_TIPLERI = ['Dizel', 'Benzin', 'LPG', 'Elektrik', 'Diğer'];
const bosForm = {
  arac_id: '',
  yakit_tipi: 'Dizel',
  tarih: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  litre: '',
  tutar: '',
  km: '',
  notlar: '',
};
export default function Yakitlar() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [araclar, setAraclar] = useState([]);
  const [yakitlar, setYakitlar] = useState([]);
  const [seciliAracId, setSeciliAracId] = useState('tum');
  const [aramaMetni, setAramaMetni] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
````
