````javascript
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingYakitId, setEditingYakitId] = useState(null);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState(bosForm);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [deleteYakitId, setDeleteYakitId] = useState(null);
  const verileriYukle = useCallback(
    async (aracFiltre = seciliAracId) => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const aracRes = await aracService.getAraclar();
        const aracListesi = Array.isArray(aracRes) ? aracRes : aracRes?.araclar || [];
        setAraclar(aracListesi);
        if (aracFiltre && aracFiltre !== 'tum') {
          const yakitRes = await yakitService.getYakitlarByArac(aracFiltre);
          setYakitlar(Array.isArray(yakitRes) ? yakitRes : yakitRes?.yakitlar || []);
        } else {
          const yakitRes = await yakitService.getAllYakitlar();
          setYakitlar(Array.isArray(yakitRes) ? yakitRes : yakitRes?.yakitlar || []);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Yakıt kayıtları yüklenemedi.');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [seciliAracId]
  );
  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (!token) {
      navigate('/login');
      return;
    }
    verileriYukle(seciliAracId);
  }, [navigate, seciliAracId, verileriYukle]);
  const gosterilenYakitlar = useMemo(() => {
    const q = aramaMetni.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return yakitlar;
    return yakitlar.filter(
      (y) =>
        (y.plaka || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (y.yakit_tipi || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (y.notlar || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (y.marka || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (y.model || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [yakitlar, aramaMetni]);
  const ozet = useMemo(() => {
    const toplamTutar = gosterilenYakitlar.reduce((sum, y) => sum + (Number(y.tutar) || 0), 0);
    const toplamLitre = gosterilenYakitlar.reduce((sum, y) => sum + (Number(y.litre) || 0), 0);
    const ortalamaBirimFiyat = toplamLitre > 0 ? toplamTutar / toplamLitre : 0;
    return { toplamTutar, toplamLitre, ortalamaBirimFiyat };
  }, [gosterilenYakitlar]);
  const openAddModal = () => {
    setEditingYakitId(null);
    setFormData({
      ...bosForm,
      arac_id:
        seciliAracId !== 'tum'
          ? String(seciliAracId)
          : araclar[0]?.arac_id
            ? String(araclar[0].arac_id)
            : '',
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };
  const openEditModal = (yakit) => {
    setEditingYakitId(yakit.yakit_id);
    setFormData({
      arac_id: String(yakit.arac_id || ''),
      yakit_tipi: yakit.yakit_tipi || 'Dizel',
      tarih: yakit.tarih ? String(yakit.tarih).slice(0, 10) : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      litre: yakit.litre ?? '',
      tutar: yakit.tutar ?? '',
      km: yakit.km ?? '',
      notlar: yakit.notlar || '',
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };
  const closeModal = () => {
    setIsModalOpen(false);
    setEditingYakitId(null);
    setFormData(bosForm);
  };
  const handleInputChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
  };
  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formData.arac_id || !formData.tarih || formData.litre === '' || formData.tutar === '') {
      toast.error('Lütfen zorunlu alanları (Araç, Tarih, Litre, Tutar) doldurun.');
      return;
    }
    const payload = {
      arac_id: Number(formData.arac_id),
      yakit_tipi: formData.yakit_tipi || 'Diğer',
      tarih: formData.tarih,
      litre: Number(formData.litre),
      tutar: Number(formData.tutar),
      km: formData.km === '' || formData.km === null ? null : Number(formData.km),
      notlar: formData.notlar.trim() || null,
    };
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (editingYakitId) {
        await yakitService.yakitGuncelle(editingYakitId, payload);
        toast.success('Yakıt kaydı güncellendi.');
      } else {
        await yakitService.yakitEkle(payload);
        toast.success('Yakıt kaydı eklendi.');
      }
      closeModal();
      await verileriYukle(seciliAracId);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Yakıt kaydedilemedi.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };
  const handleDelete = async () => {
    try {
      await yakitService.yakitSil(deleteYakitId);
      setYakitlar((prev) => prev.filter((y) => y.yakit_id !== deleteYakitId));
      toast.success('Yakıt kaydı silindi.');
      setDeleteYakitId(null);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Yakıt kaydı silinemedi.');
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Kompakt Filtre ve Aksiyon Çubuğu (Toolbar) */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Car className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <select
            value={seciliAracId}
            onChange={(e) => setSeciliAracId(e.target.value)}
            className="h-8 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2.5 text-xs text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring"
          >
            <option value="tum">Tüm Araçlar</option>
            {araclar.map((a) => (
              <option key={a.arac_id} value={a.arac_id}>
                {a.plaka} — {a.marka} {a.model}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-60">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-2 size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
            <Input
              type="search"
              placeholder="Plaka, yakıt tipi veya not ara…"
              value={aramaMetni}
              onChange={(e) => setAramaMetni(e.target.value)}
              className="h-8 pl-8 text-xs"
            />
          </div>
          <Button size="sm" className="h-8 gap-1.5 text-xs" onClick={openAddModal}>
            <Plus className="size-3.5" /> Yeni Yakıt Kaydı
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Kompakt Tek Satırlık KPI Şeridi */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Banknote className="size-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
            Toplam Harcama
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">
            {ozet.toplamTutar.toLocaleString('tr-TR', {
              minimumFractionDigits: 2,
              maximumFractionDigits: 2,
            })}{' '}
            ₺
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Droplets className="size-4 shrink-0 text-sky-500" />
            Toplam Litre
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">
            {ozet.toplamLitre.toLocaleString('tr-TR', {
              minimumFractionDigits: 2,
              maximumFractionDigits: 2,
            })}{' '}
            L
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            <Fuel className="size-4 shrink-0 text-amber-500" />
            Ortalama Litre Fiyatı
          </span>
          <span className="text-base font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight">
            {ozet.ortalamaBirimFiyat.toLocaleString('tr-TR', {
              minimumFractionDigits: 2,
              maximumFractionDigits: 2,
            })}{' '}
            ₺/L
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Tablo */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-muted/40" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : gosterilenYakitlar.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-border py-16 text-center text-muted-foreground">
          <Fuel className="mx-auto mb-3 size-8 opacity-40" />
          <p className="text-sm font-medium">Henüz yakıt kaydı bulunmuyor.</p>
          <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="mt-3 gap-1.5" onClick={openAddModal}>
            <Plus className="size-4" /> İlk Yakıt Kaydını Ekle
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border">
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow className="bg-muted/30">
                <TableHead>Plaka / Araç</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Yakıt Tipi</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Tarih</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Litre</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Tutar</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Kilometre</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Notlar</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="text-right">İşlemler</TableHead>
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {gosterilenYakitlar.map((yakit) => (
                <TableRow key={yakit.yakit_id} className="hover:bg-muted/30">
                  <TableCell className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold">
                    <div>{yakit.plaka || `#${yakit.arac_id}`}</div>
                    {(yakit.marka || yakit.model) && (
                      <div className="font-sans text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                        {yakit.marka} {yakit.model}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1">
                      <Fuel className="size-3" />
                      {yakit.yakit_tipi || 'Diğer'}
                    </Badge>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-sm">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Calendar className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                      {yakit.tarih ? new Date(yakit.tarih).toLocaleDateString('tr-TR') : '-'}
                    </span>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-sm font-medium">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Droplets className="size-3.5 text-sky-500" />
                      {Number(yakit.litre || 0).toLocaleString('tr-TR', {
                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                      })}{' '}
                      L
                    </span>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-sm font-semibold">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Banknote className="size-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
                      {Number(yakit.tutar || 0).toLocaleString('tr-TR', {
                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                      })}{' '}
                      ₺
                    </span>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-sm">
                    {yakit.km !== null && yakit.km !== undefined ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Gauge className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                        {Number(yakit.km).toLocaleString('tr-TR')} KM
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">-</span>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {yakit.notlar || '-'}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-right">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                      <Button
                        size="icon"
                        variant="ghost"
                        className="size-7"
                        onClick={() => openEditModal(yakit)}
                      >
                        <Pencil className="size-3.5" />
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        size="icon"
                        variant="ghost"
                        className="size-7 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                        onClick={() => setDeleteYakitId(yakit.yakit_id)}
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
        </div>
      )}
      <ConfirmAlertDialog
        open={deleteYakitId !== null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setDeleteYakitId(null)}
        title="Yakıt kaydını sil?"
        description="Bu yakıt kaydı kalıcı olarak silinecek."
        onConfirm={handleDelete}
      />
      {/* Ekle / Düzenle Modalı */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 px-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white/95 p-6 text-slate-900 dark:border-white/10 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 dark:text-zinc-100 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pb-3">
              <h3 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                <Fuel className="size-4 text-primary" />
                {editingYakitId ? 'Yakıt Kaydını Düzenle' : 'Yeni Yakıt Kaydı'}
              </h3>
              <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" className="size-7 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-white" onClick={closeModal}>
                <X className="size-4" />
              </Button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Araç</Label>
                <select
                  name="arac_id"
                  value={formData.arac_id}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  required
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                >
                  <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">Araç seçin…</option>
                  {araclar.map((a) => (
                    <option key={a.arac_id} value={a.arac_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                      {a.plaka} — {a.marka} {a.model}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Yakıt Tipi</Label>
                  <select
                    name="yakit_tipi"
                    value={formData.yakit_tipi}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                  >
                    {YAKIT_TIPLERI.map((tip) => (
                      <option key={tip} value={tip} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                        {tip}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Tarih</Label>
                  <TrussDatePicker
                    name="tarih"
                    value={formData.tarih}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    placeholder="Yakıt tarihi seçin"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Litre (L)</Label>
                  <Input
                    type="number"
                    step="0.01"
                    min="0"
                    name="litre"
                    value={formData.litre}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    placeholder="45.50"
                    className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Tutar (₺)</Label>
                  <Input
                    type="number"
                    step="0.01"
                    min="0"
                    name="tutar"
                    value={formData.tutar}
                    onChange={handleInputChange}
                    placeholder="1950.00"
                    className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Kilometre (KM)</Label>
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  name="km"
                  value={formData.km}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  placeholder="125000 (İsteğe bağlı)"
                  className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  <FileText className="size-3.5" /> Notlar (İsteğe Bağlı)
                </Label>
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  name="notlar"
                  value={formData.notlar}
                  onChange={handleInputChange}
                  placeholder="İstasyon adı, fiş no vb."
                  className="h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 pt-3">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={closeModal}
                  className="border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-700/80 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:border-primary/50 dark:hover:bg-primary/15 dark:hover:text-white"
                >
                  İptal
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting}>
                  {isSubmitting
                    ? 'Kaydediliyor…'
                    : editingYakitId
                      ? 'Güncelle'
                      : 'Kaydet'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

````javascript
import api from './api';
const adminService = {
  getUsers: async () => {
    const response = await api.get('/admin/users');
    return response.data?.users || [];
  },
  createDriver: async (driverData) => {
    const response = await api.post('/admin/users', driverData);
    return response.data;
  },
  assignVehicle: async (assignData) => {
    const response = await api.post('/admin/assign', assignData);
    return response.data;
  },
};
export default adminService;
````

## File: Frontend/src/services/katalogService.js
````javascript
import api from './api';
const katalogService = {
  getKategoriler: async () => {
    const response = await api.get('/katalog/kategoriler');
    return response.data?.kategoriler || [];
  },
  getMarkalar: async (kategori_id) => {
    const params = kategori_id ? { kategori_id } : {};
    const response = await api.get('/katalog/markalar', { params });
    return response.data?.markalar || [];
  },
  getModeller: async (marka_id, kategori_id) => {
    if (!marka_id) return [];
    const params = { marka_id };
    if (kategori_id) params.kategori_id = kategori_id;
    const response = await api.get('/katalog/modeller', { params });
    return response.data?.modeller || [];
  },
  getVaryantlar: async (model_id) => {
    if (!model_id) return [];
    const response = await api.get('/katalog/varyantlar', {
      params: { model_id },
    });
    return response.data?.varyantlar || [];
  },
};
export default katalogService;
````

## File: Frontend/src/store/useAuthStore.js
````javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import authService from '@/services/authService';
const TOKEN_KEY = 'token';
const USER_KEY = 'user';
const readUser = () => {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY)) || null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};
const useAuthStore = create((set) => ({
  token: localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY),
  user: readUser(),
  isAuthenticated: Boolean(localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)),
  setSession: (token, user) => {
    localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);
    localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user || null));
    set({ token, user: user || null, isAuthenticated: true });
  },
  login: async (credentials) => {
    const data = await authService.login(credentials);
    if (!data?.token) {
      throw new Error('Sunucudan token alınamadı.');
    }
    useAuthStore.getState().setSession(data.token, data.user);
    return data;
  },
  logout: () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
    set({ token: null, user: null, isAuthenticated: false });
  },
}));
export default useAuthStore;
````

## File: Frontend/src/store/useVehicleStore.js
````javascript
import { create } from 'zustand';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import api from '@/services/api';
import aracService from '@/services/aracService';
import { formatTurkishPlate } from '@/utils/formatters';
const MILLISECONDS_IN_DAY = 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24;
const EVRAK_UYARI_GUN = 15;
const bosForm = {
  plaka: '',
  kategori_id: '',
  kategori: '',
  marka: '',
  model: '',
  yil: '',
  muayene_tarihi: '',
  kasko_tarihi: '',
  sigorta_tarihi: '',
  onerilen_bakim_km: null,
  triger_degisim_km: null,
  calisma_saati_bakim: null,
};
const bosRisk = { tehlike: [], evrak: [] };
const tarihInput = (deger) => (deger ? String(deger).slice(0, 10) : '');
const evrakRiskliMi = (arac) => {
  const bugun = new Date();
  bugun.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return [arac.muayene_tarihi, arac.kasko_tarihi, arac.sigorta_tarihi].some((t) => {
    if (!t) return false;
    const hedef = new Date(t);
    hedef.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    return Math.ceil((hedef - bugun) / MILLISECONDS_IN_DAY) <= EVRAK_UYARI_GUN;
  });
};
let modalKapanmaTimer = null;
const useVehicleStore = create((set, get) => ({
  // Liste
  araclar: [],
  vehicles: [],
  loading: false,
  hasLoaded: false,
  error: null,
  riskliAracIdler: bosRisk,
  // Ekle/Düzenle modalı
  isModalOpen: false,
  isModalVisible: false,
  editingAracId: null,
  formData: bosForm,
  isSubmitting: false,
  fetchAraclar: async () => {
    set({ loading: true, error: null });
    try {
      const data = await aracService.getAraclar();
      const liste = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.araclar || [];
      const tehlike = [];
      const evrak = [];
      liste.forEach((arac) => {
        if (evrakRiskliMi(arac)) evrak.push(arac.arac_id);
        const isSmh = arac.sayac_tipi === 'SMH' || Number(arac.calisma_saati_bakim) > 0;
        const sayac = Number(
          arac.guncel_sayac ?? (isSmh ? arac.guncel_calisma_saati : arac.guncel_km) ?? 0
        );
        const periyot = Number(
          (isSmh ? arac.calisma_saati_bakim : arac.onerilen_bakim_km) ||
          arac.bakim_periyodu ||
          (isSmh ? 250 : 15000)
        );
        const sonBakim =
          arac.son_bakim_km !== null && arac.son_bakim_km !== undefined
            ? Number(arac.son_bakim_km)
            : null;
        if (sonBakim !== null && Number.isFinite(sonBakim) && Number.isFinite(periyot) && sayac >= sonBakim + periyot) {
          tehlike.push(arac.arac_id);
        }
      });
      set({ araclar: liste, vehicles: liste, riskliAracIdler: { tehlike, evrak }, hasLoaded: true });
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err?.response?.data?.message || 'Araçlar yüklenemedi.';
      set({ error: message });
      toast.error(message);
    } finally {
      set({ loading: false });
    }
  },
  openModal: () => {
    if (modalKapanmaTimer) clearTimeout(modalKapanmaTimer);
    set({ editingAracId: null, formData: bosForm, isModalOpen: true, isModalVisible: true });
  },
  openEditModal: (arac) => {
    if (modalKapanmaTimer) clearTimeout(modalKapanmaTimer);
    set({
      editingAracId: arac.arac_id,
      formData: {
        plaka: formatTurkishPlate(arac.plaka || ''),
        kategori_id: arac.kategori_id ? String(arac.kategori_id) : '',
        kategori: arac.kategori || '',
        marka: arac.marka || '',
        model: arac.model || '',
        yil: arac.yil || '',
        muayene_tarihi: tarihInput(arac.muayene_tarihi),
        kasko_tarihi: tarihInput(arac.kasko_tarihi),
        sigorta_tarihi: tarihInput(arac.sigorta_tarihi),
        onerilen_bakim_km: null,
        triger_degisim_km: null,
        calisma_saati_bakim: null,
      },
      isModalOpen: true,
      isModalVisible: true,
    });
  },
  closeModal: () => {
    set({ isModalVisible: false });
    if (modalKapanmaTimer) clearTimeout(modalKapanmaTimer);
    modalKapanmaTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      set({ isModalOpen: false, editingAracId: null, formData: bosForm });
      modalKapanmaTimer = null;
    }, 200);
  },
  setFormField: (name, value) => {
    set((state) => ({ formData: { ...state.formData, [name]: value } }));
  },
  submitArac: async () => {
    const { formData, editingAracId, closeModal, fetchAraclar } = get();
    if (!formData.plaka || !formData.marka || !formData.model || !formData.yil) {
      toast.error('Lütfen tüm alanları doldurun.');
      return false;
    }
    const payload = {
      plaka: formData.plaka,
      kategori_id: formData.kategori_id ? Number(formData.kategori_id) : null,
      kategori: formData.kategori || null,
      marka: formData.marka,
      model: formData.model,
      yil: Number(formData.yil),
      muayene_tarihi: formData.muayene_tarihi || null,
      kasko_tarihi: formData.kasko_tarihi || null,
      sigorta_tarihi: formData.sigorta_tarihi || null,
    };
    set({ isSubmitting: true });
    try {
      if (editingAracId) await aracService.aracGuncelle(editingAracId, payload);
      else await aracService.aracEkle(payload);
      toast.success(editingAracId ? 'Araç güncellendi.' : 'Araç eklendi.');
      closeModal();
      await fetchAraclar();
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'İşlem başarısız.');
      return false;
    } finally {
      set({ isSubmitting: false });
    }
  },
  deleteVehicle: async (id) => {
    try {
      await api.delete(`/araclar/${id}`);
      set((state) => ({
        vehicles: state.vehicles.filter((v) => v.arac_id !== id && v.id !== id),
        araclar: state.araclar.filter((a) => a.arac_id !== id && a.id !== id),
        riskliAracIdler: {
          tehlike: state.riskliAracIdler.tehlike.filter((rid) => rid !== id),
          evrak: state.riskliAracIdler.evrak.filter((rid) => rid !== id),
        },
      }));
      toast.success('Araç silindi.');
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Araç silinemedi.');
      return false;
    }
  },
  deleteArac: async (aracId) => {
    return get().deleteVehicle(aracId);
  },
  // Çıkışta bir önceki kullanıcının araçları bellekte kalmasın.
  reset: () => {
    if (modalKapanmaTimer) clearTimeout(modalKapanmaTimer);
    modalKapanmaTimer = null;
    set({
      araclar: [],
      vehicles: [],
      loading: false,
      hasLoaded: false,
      error: null,
      riskliAracIdler: bosRisk,
      isModalOpen: false,
      isModalVisible: false,
      editingAracId: null,
      formData: bosForm,
      isSubmitting: false,
    });
  },
}));
export default useVehicleStore;
````

## File: Frontend/src/utils/aracDurum.js
````javascript
const MILLISECONDS_IN_DAY = 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24;
export const EVRAK_UYARI_GUN = 15;
const VARSAYILAN_KM_PERIYODU = 15000;
const VARSAYILAN_SMH_PERIYODU = 250;
const UYARI_ORANI = 0.1; // Periyodun son %10'u "yaklaşıyor" kabul edilir
const sayi = (deger) => {
  const n = Number(deger);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
};
export const isSmhArac = (arac) => arac?.sayac_tipi === 'SMH';
export const getSayacBirimi = (arac) => (isSmhArac(arac) ? 'saat' : 'km');
export const getBakimPeriyodu = (arac) => {
  const backend = sayi(arac?.bakim_periyodu);
  if (backend && backend > 0) return backend;
  if (isSmhArac(arac)) {
    const smh = sayi(arac?.calisma_saati_bakim);
    return smh && smh > 0 ? smh : VARSAYILAN_SMH_PERIYODU;
  }
  const km = sayi(arac?.onerilen_bakim_km);
  return km && km > 0 ? km : VARSAYILAN_KM_PERIYODU;
};
export const getGuncelSayac = (arac) => {
  const backend = sayi(arac?.guncel_sayac);
  if (backend !== null) return backend;
  return (isSmhArac(arac) ? sayi(arac?.guncel_calisma_saati) : sayi(arac?.guncel_km)) || 0;
};
/**
 * Aracın periyodik bakım durumunu, kendi varyant periyoduna (onerilen_bakim_km / calisma_saati_bakim) göre hesaplar.
 * @returns {{ durum: 'tehlike'|'uyari'|'sorunsuz'|'bilinmiyor', kalan: number|null, periyot: number, birim: string }}
 */
export const hesaplaBakimDurumu = (arac) => {
  const periyot = getBakimPeriyodu(arac);
  const birim = getSayacBirimi(arac);
  const sonBakim = sayi(arac?.son_bakim_km);
  if (sonBakim === null) {
    return { durum: 'bilinmiyor', kalan: null, periyot, birim };
  }
  const kalan = sonBakim + periyot - getGuncelSayac(arac);
  if (kalan <= 0) return { durum: 'tehlike', kalan, periyot, birim };
  if (kalan <= periyot * UYARI_ORANI) return { durum: 'uyari', kalan, periyot, birim };
  return { durum: 'sorunsuz', kalan, periyot, birim };
};
export const evrakRiskliMi = (arac, gun = EVRAK_UYARI_GUN) => {
  const bugun = new Date();
  bugun.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return [arac?.muayene_tarihi, arac?.kasko_tarihi, arac?.sigorta_tarihi].some((t) => {
    if (!t) return false;
    const hedef = new Date(t);
    hedef.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    return Math.ceil((hedef - bugun) / MILLISECONDS_IN_DAY) <= gun;
  });
};
export const ozetleFilo = (araclar = []) =>
  araclar.reduce(
    (acc, arac) => {
      const { durum } = hesaplaBakimDurumu(arac);
      if (durum === 'tehlike') {
        acc.tehlike += 1;
        acc.tehlikeIdler.push(arac.arac_id);
      } else if (durum === 'uyari') {
        acc.uyari += 1;
        acc.uyariIdler.push(arac.arac_id);
      } else {
        acc.sorunsuz += 1;
      }
      if (evrakRiskliMi(arac)) {
        acc.evrakSorunu += 1;
        acc.evrakIdler.push(arac.arac_id);
      }
      acc.gelecekAyTahmin += Number(arac?.son_30_gun_yakit_tutari) || 0;
      return acc;
    },
    {
      tehlike: 0,
      uyari: 0,
      sorunsuz: 0,
      evrakSorunu: 0,
      gelecekAyTahmin: 0,
      tehlikeIdler: [],
      uyariIdler: [],
      evrakIdler: [],
    }
  );
````

## File: skills/system/run-workflow/SKILL.md
````markdown
---
name: run-workflow
description: 'How to write and run a dynamic workflow script with the `run_workflow` tool: a Python script that orchestrates many agents with `agent`, `parallel`, `pipeline`, and `phase`. Use when a task splits into many independent or staged pieces of agent work (fan out over files, modules, bugs, or review angles, then aggregate), or when the user asks for a workflow. Don''t use for a single delegated task (use a subagent) or for legacy workflow files (see migrate-workflows).'
metadata:
    namespace: system
---
# Dynamic workflows with `run_workflow`

A workflow is a short Python script that the `run_workflow` tool executes. The
script decides which agents to start, in what order, and what to do with their
results. Use it instead of many manual subagent calls when the orchestration
itself is code: loops over a list, fan-out then aggregate, or staged pipelines.

## Steps

1.  Pass the script inline as `Script`, or write it to a file (for example in
    the conversation scratch directory) and pass its absolute path as
    `ScriptPath`. Set exactly one of them; a file is easier to fix and rerun.
2.  Call `run_workflow(Script="...", Description="...")` or
    `run_workflow(ScriptPath="/abs/path/workflow.py", Description="...")`.
    `Description` is a one-line goal shown in the UI.
    The workflow runs in the background, and you are notified when it ends.
    For a quick script, such as a `call_tool` fan-out, set
    `WaitMsBeforeAsync` (up to 10000) to get the result in the same turn if it
    finishes in time.
    To pass inputs, set `Args` to a JSON object; the script reads it as the
    `args` dict. When `ScriptPath` is a saved workflow's entry script, `Args`
    is validated against the args schema in its `workflow.json`.
3.  Read the tool result: it contains the `log()` output (`Logs:`) and what the
    script printed (`Output:`). Each agent is also linked from the workflow
    step, so the user can open its conversation.
4.  If the tool returns an error, fix the script at the reported line and run
    it again.

## Script rules

The script runs in its own `python3` process, which needs Python 3.8 or later
on `PATH`:

-   `await` works at the top level, and you can define `async def` helpers.
-   Installed library workflows are importable by name (`import <library>` or
    `from <library> import <name>`); library modules see the same primitives as
    the script.
-   What the script prints (`print()`, `stderr`, child-process output, and the
    `repr` of a trailing expression) is returned in the tool result's `Output:`
    section (and appended to the error when the workflow fails).
-   An uncaught exception stops the workflow and reports the script line.
-   `CONVERSATION_ID` and `STEP_INDEX` identify the `run_workflow` step.
    `WORKSPACES` is a tuple of the workspace directories, primary first (empty
    if there are none).
-   The working directory is `Cwd` if given, else `WORKSPACES[0]`, so relative
    paths resolve against it. Local modules are not importable.

## Primitives

| Primitive | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| `log(*args)` | Adds a line to the workflow output and the UI. |
| `phase(name)` | Starts a named phase; later logs and agents group under it. |
| `agent(prompt=None, schema=None, role=None, type_name="self", workspace="inherit", model=None)` | Defines an agent; nothing runs yet. `await agent(prompt, schema)` runs it once and returns its result. `a = agent(...)` then `await a(prompt, schema)` runs one turn in a conversation kept across calls. |
| `parallel(items, fn)` | Promise that calls `fn(item)` for each item and awaits all results concurrently; results are in item order. If one fails, the rest are cancelled. |
| `pipeline(items, fn)` | Promise that calls and awaits `fn(item)` one item at a time; results are in item order. |
| `trajectory(last=None, types=None, tools=None, min_index=None, max_index=None)` | `await trajectory()` returns steps before this `run_workflow` call started, oldest first. `min_index` and `max_index` bound `step.index` (inclusive); `types` and `tools` filter by step type or tool name; `last` keeps the last N matching steps. |

Arguments:

-   `type_name`: the agent type to run. `"self"` uses the current agent's
    configuration; any available custom agent name also works.
-   `workspace`: `"inherit"` (the current workspace), `"branch"` (a new isolated
    clone, for agents that edit code in parallel), or `"share"` (a new workspace
    that shares the underlying repository). Other values are errors.
-   `schema` (required): a JSON schema (dict or string) for the agent's result.
    The agent returns the result by calling its `finish` tool, which checks it
    against the schema, and `await agent(...)` returns the parsed value. An
    agent that ends without calling `finish` fails the workflow. Use
    `{"type": "string"}` for a free-form answer.
-   `role`: an optional short title for the agent's card, such as
    `"Security reviewer"`.
-   `model`: the agent's model tier: `"inherit"` (default, your model),
    `"flash_lite"`, `"flash"`, or `"pro"`. Use a lighter tier for simple,
    high-fan-out work.
-   `fn`: usually a `lambda` that returns `agent(...)`, or an `async def`
    helper that awaits several agents. It must return something awaitable:
    an `agent()` or a call on one, a nested `parallel()` / `pipeline()`, or
    an `async def` call. Build per-item prompts and roles with f-strings.

Awaiting the same agent twice runs it once and returns the same result. An
`agent()` that is never awaited or called never runs, so always `await` it, or
pass it to `parallel` / `pipeline` and await that.

The workflow step shows one card per agent, in the order agents start, titled
with its `role`, or else the first line of its prompt, so set a role or start
each prompt with a short line that names the job.

## Agents that keep their context

When one agent should do several related turns, such as a reviewer that checks
the fixes for the bugs it found, call it instead of awaiting it. Each
`a(prompt, schema)` is lazy like an awaited `agent()` and returns that turn's
`finish` result; the agent's own `prompt`, if any, is its standing instructions
and leads the first turn. `schema` on a call defaults to the agent's. Turns on
one agent run one at a time, in the order they were started. A failed turn
raises, but the agent stays usable. An agent is either awaited or called, not
both. `del a` ends the agent, unless something else, such as a lambda, still
references it; every agent ends when the workflow ends.

```python
reviewer = agent("You review Go code for correctness.", role="Reviewer")
bugs = await reviewer("List the bugs in foo.go.", {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}})
fixes = await parallel(bugs, lambda b: agent(f"Fix: {b}", {"type": "string"}))
ok = await reviewer(f"Fixes made: {fixes}. Are all your bugs fixed?", {"type": "boolean"})
del reviewer
```

## Calling tools from the script

Where the `run_workflow` tool description lists tools for `call_tool`, the
script can call them directly:

-   `call_tool(name, **args)` returns a promise like `agent()` does. Awaiting it
    runs the tool with the arguments you would pass to it directly and returns
    the tool's output as a `str`.
-   It raises `ToolError` (with `.tool` and `.message`) if the tool fails or
    the call is denied. `.denied` is `True` when a pre-tool hook or the
    permission check rejected the call before the tool ran, so a script can
    skip denied calls and still fail on real errors.
-   The user cannot be asked anything from a workflow: a call that needs user
    approval that has not already been granted is denied.
-   Tool outputs are not shown to you unless the script logs or prints them.

## Reading earlier conversation steps

Pass inputs you already have in `Args` (or inline in the script). Use
`await trajectory(...)` when the script needs to inspect steps from earlier in
this conversation—such as mining a failed `run_command` output without
rerunning it—and pass only the relevant slice into child `agent()` prompts.

Each returned `Step` is a `dict` that also supports attribute access (missing
fields return `None` as attributes):

-   `index` (`int`): 0-based step index.
-   `type` (`str`): step type, e.g. `"USER_INPUT"`, `"PLANNER_RESPONSE"`,
    `"SYSTEM_MESSAGE"`, or `"GENERIC"` (tool calls).
-   `status` (`str`): e.g. `"DONE"`, `"ERROR"`, or `"CLEARED"`.
-   Message steps set `text` (`str`); tool steps set `tool` (`str`), `args`
    (parsed `dict`), and `output` (`str`, truncated at 200k characters).

```python
steps = await trajectory(tools=["run_command"], last=20)
errors = [s.output for s in steps if s.status == "ERROR" and s.output]
log(f"Found {len(errors)} failed command(s)")
```

`trajectory()` only returns steps before the `run_workflow` call started (steps
added while the workflow runs in the background are not included). Older tool
steps evicted from memory have `status == "CLEARED"` and `output == None`; read
the conversation's `transcript.jsonl` / `transcript_full.jsonl` when you need
cleared tool outputs or steps added after the workflow started.

## Behavior to know

-   Workflow agents are main agents, not subagents. They are hidden from the
    conversation list and reachable from the workflow step. An awaited agent
    is single-use; a called agent lasts until deleted or the workflow ends.
-   `await agent(...)` returns when the agent's run ends, with the result it
    passed to `finish`.
-   If one agent in `parallel` fails, the whole call fails and the other agents
    in it are cancelled. Stopping the parent conversation stops all running
    workflow agents.
-   Agents do not see the script or each other. Put all the context an agent
    needs in its prompt, and ask for a short, well-defined output.

## Example

Fan out over a list that an agent produces:

```python
phase("Discover")
files = await agent(
    "List the Go files under pkg/foo that call Bar(). Paths only.",
    schema={"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
)

phase("Review")
reviews = await parallel(files, lambda f: agent(
    f"Review {f} for misuse of Bar(). Reply in 3 bullets or 'OK'.",
    schema={"type": "string"},
    role=f"Review {f}",
))
for r in reviews:
    log(r)
```
````

## File: Backend/middlewares/authMiddleware.js
````javascript
const jwt = require('jsonwebtoken'); // JWT token doğrulamak için kütüphaneyi dahil eder.
const authMiddleware = (req, res, next) => { // Korunan rotalar için kimlik doğrulama ara katmanını tanımlar.
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization; // Authorization başlığını alır.
  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) { // Başlık yoksa veya Bearer ile başlamıyorsa kontrol eder.
    return res.status(401).json({ message: 'Token bulunamadı' }); // Token yoksa 401 Unauthorized döner.
  }
  const token = authHeader.split(' ')[1]; // Bearer kısmını atıp token değerini ayırır.
  const secret = process.env.JWT_SECRET || 'gizli_anahtar_gecici'; // JWT secret'ı .env'den alır, yoksa varsayılanı kullanır.
  try {
    req.user = jwt.verify(token, secret); // Token'ı doğrular ve çözülen payload'ı req.user içine yazar.
    next(); // İstek akışını bir sonraki adıma geçirir.
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(403).json({ message: 'Geçersiz veya süresi dolmuş token' }); // Hatalı veya süresi dolmuş token için 403 döner.
  }
};
module.exports = authMiddleware; // Middleware fonksiyonunu dışa aktarır.
````

## File: Backend/routes/parcaRoutes.js
````javascript
const express = require('express');
const authMiddleware = require('../middlewares/authMiddleware');
const { authorizeRoles } = require('../middlewares/rbacMiddleware');
const {
  getAllParcalar,
  getParcalarByArac,
  createParca,
  updateParca,
  deleteParca,
} = require('../controllers/parcaController');
const router = express.Router();
router.get('/', authMiddleware, getAllParcalar);
router.get('/arac/:arac_id', authMiddleware, getParcalarByArac);
router.post('/', authMiddleware, authorizeRoles('SYSTEM_ADMIN', 'FLEET_MANAGER'), createParca);
router.put('/:parca_id', authMiddleware, authorizeRoles('SYSTEM_ADMIN', 'FLEET_MANAGER'), updateParca);
router.delete('/:parca_id', authMiddleware, authorizeRoles('SYSTEM_ADMIN', 'FLEET_MANAGER'), deleteParca);
module.exports = router;
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/dashboard/ChartAreaInteractive.jsx
````javascript
import * as React from 'react'
import { Area, AreaChart, CartesianGrid, XAxis, YAxis } from '@/components/ui/chart'
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { ChartContainer, ChartTooltip, ChartTooltipContent, ChartLegend, ChartLegendContent } from '@/components/ui/chart'
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select'
const chartConfig = {
  toplam_yakit: { label: 'Yakıt (TL)', color: 'var(--chart-1)' },
  toplam_bakim: { label: 'Bakım (TL)', color: 'var(--chart-2)' },
}
const AY_ETIKET = {
  '01': 'Oca', '02': 'Şub', '03': 'Mar', '04': 'Nis',
  '05': 'May', '06': 'Haz', '07': 'Tem', '08': 'Ağu',
  '09': 'Eyl', '10': 'Eki', '11': 'Kas', '12': 'Ara',
}
function formatAy(ayStr) {
  if (!ayStr) return ''
  const [, mm] = ayStr.split('-')
  return AY_ETIKET[mm] ?? ayStr
}
export function ChartAreaInteractive({ data = [] }) {
  const [aralik, setAralik] = React.useState('6ay')
  const filtered = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (!data.length) return []
    const sorted = [...data].sort((a, b) => a.ay.localeCompare(b.ay))
    if (aralik === '3ay') return sorted.slice(-3)
    if (aralik === '1ay') return sorted.slice(-1)
    return sorted
  }, [data, aralik])
  const chartRows = filtered.map(r => ({
    ay: formatAy(r.ay),
    toplam_yakit: Math.round(Number(r.toplam_yakit) || 0),
    toplam_bakim: Math.round(Number(r.toplam_bakim) || 0),
  }))
  const hasData = chartRows.some(r => r.toplam_yakit > 0 || r.toplam_bakim > 0)
  return (
    <Card className="mb-6">
      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-start justify-between gap-4 pb-2">
        <div>
          <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold">Aylık Gider Trendi</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Yakıt ve bakım giderlerinin aylık dağılımı</CardDescription>
        </div>
        <Select value={aralik} onValueChange={setAralik}>
          <SelectTrigger className="h-8 w-32 text-xs">
            <SelectValue />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            <SelectItem value="6ay">Son 6 ay</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="3ay">Son 3 ay</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="1ay">Bu ay</SelectItem>
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="pt-2">
        {!hasData ? (
          <div className="flex h-[260px] items-center justify-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Bu dönem için henüz gider kaydı bulunmuyor.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <ChartContainer config={chartConfig} className="h-[260px] w-full">
            <AreaChart data={chartRows} margin={{ top: 10, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 0 }}>
              <defs>
                <linearGradient id="yakitGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                  <stop offset="5%" stopColor="var(--chart-1)" stopOpacity={0.3} />
                  <stop offset="95%" stopColor="var(--chart-1)" stopOpacity={0.02} />
                </linearGradient>
                <linearGradient id="bakimGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                  <stop offset="5%" stopColor="var(--chart-2)" stopOpacity={0.3} />
                  <stop offset="95%" stopColor="var(--chart-2)" stopOpacity={0.02} />
                </linearGradient>
              </defs>
              <CartesianGrid vertical={false} strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="var(--border)" strokeOpacity={0.4} />
              <XAxis dataKey="ay" tickLine={false} axisLine={false} tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: 'var(--muted-foreground)' }} />
              <YAxis tickLine={false} axisLine={false} tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: 'var(--muted-foreground)' }} tickFormatter={v => v >= 1000 ? `${(v/1000).toFixed(0)}k` : v} />
              <ChartTooltip content={<ChartTooltipContent indicator="dot" />} />
              <ChartLegend content={<ChartLegendContent />} />
              <Area type="monotone" dataKey="toplam_yakit" stroke="var(--chart-1)" strokeWidth={2} fill="url(#yakitGrad)" dot={false} />
              <Area type="monotone" dataKey="toplam_bakim" stroke="var(--chart-2)" strokeWidth={2} fill="url(#bakimGrad)" dot={false} />
            </AreaChart>
          </ChartContainer>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/layout/AppLayout.jsx
````javascript
import React, { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react'
import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useShallow } from 'zustand/react/shallow'
import Sidebar, { MobileSidebar } from './Sidebar'
import SettingsModal from '@/components/SettingsModal'
import useAppStore from '@/store/useAppStore'
const routeTitleMap = {
  '/dashboard': 'Dashboard',
  '/vehicles': 'Araçlar',
  '/yakit': 'Yakıt',
  '/yakitlar': 'Yakıt',
  '/bakim': 'Bakım',
  '/bakimlar': 'Bakım',
  '/parcalar': 'Parça Takibi',
  '/parts': 'Parça Takibi',
  '/raporlar': 'Raporlar',
  '/reports': 'Raporlar',
  '/ekip': 'Ekip Yönetimi',
  '/settings': 'Ayarlar',
}
export default function AppLayout({ children }) {
  const location = useLocation()
  const title = useMemo(() => {
    if (location.pathname.startsWith('/arac/') || location.pathname.startsWith('/vehicle/')) {
      return 'Araç Detay'
    }
    return routeTitleMap[location.pathname] || 'Dashboard'
  }, [location.pathname])
  const { syncPreferenceSummary, cleanupSettingsTimer } = useAppStore(
    useShallow(s => ({
      syncPreferenceSummary: s.syncPreferenceSummary,
      cleanupSettingsTimer: s.cleanupSettingsTimer,
    }))
  )
  const isAuthenticated = Boolean(localStorage.getItem('token'))
  useEffect(() => {
    syncPreferenceSummary(isAuthenticated)
  }, [isAuthenticated, syncPreferenceSummary])
  useEffect(() => () => cleanupSettingsTimer(), [cleanupSettingsTimer])
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground">
      {/* Desktop sidebar */}
      <Sidebar />
      {/* Main area — offset by sidebar width on md+ */}
      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col md:ml-60">
        {/* Top header bar */}
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 flex h-14 items-center gap-3 border-b border-border/50 bg-background/80 backdrop-blur-sm px-4 sm:px-6">
          {/* Mobile hamburger */}
          <MobileSidebar />
          {/* Dynamic Title */}
          <h1 className="font-display text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-foreground sm:text-xl">{title}</h1>
        </header>
        {/* Page content */}
        <main
          key={location.pathname}
          className="flex-1 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8"
        >
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
      <SettingsModal />
    </div>
  )
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/layout/Sidebar.jsx
````javascript
import React from 'react'
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  LayoutDashboard, Fuel, Wrench, BarChart3, Settings,
  Moon, Sun, Monitor, LogOut, Menu, Car, Cog, Users
} from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { ScrollArea } from '@/components/ui/scroll-area'
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetHeader, SheetTitle, SheetTrigger } from '@/components/ui/sheet'
import { DropdownMenu, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuItem, DropdownMenuTrigger } from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'
import { useTheme } from '@/components/theme-provider'
import useAppStore from '@/store/useAppStore'
import useAuthStore from '@/store/useAuthStore'
import { useShallow } from 'zustand/react/shallow'
const linkBase = 'relative flex items-center gap-3.5 rounded-lg border-l-2 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-150'
const activeClass = 'border-primary bg-accent/50 text-foreground'
const inactiveClass = 'border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:bg-accent/40 hover:text-foreground'
function NavLink({ icon: Icon, label, href, exact = false, disabled = false, badge }) {
  const location = useLocation()
  const isActive = !disabled && (exact ? location.pathname === href : location.pathname.startsWith(href))
  return (
    <Link
      to={disabled ? '#' : href}
      onClick={e => disabled && e.preventDefault()}
      aria-current={isActive ? 'page' : undefined}
      className={cn(linkBase, isActive ? activeClass : inactiveClass, disabled && 'pointer-events-none opacity-40')}
    >
      <Icon className="size-4 shrink-0" />
      <span className="flex-1">{label}</span>
      {badge && (
        <span className="rounded-full bg-muted px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none text-muted-foreground">
          {badge}
        </span>
      )}
    </Link>
  )
}
const bottomBtnBase = 'w-full justify-start gap-3 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-accent/40 hover:text-foreground'
function SidebarContent({ onNavigate }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme()
  const { handleLogout, openSettingsModal, isSettingsOpen } = useAppStore(
    useShallow(s => ({
      handleLogout: s.handleLogout,
      openSettingsModal: s.openSettingsModal,
      isSettingsOpen: s.isSettingsOpen,
    }))
  )
  const { user } = useAuthStore()
  const userRole = user?.role || 'FLEET_MANAGER'
  const canAccessManagement = ['FLEET_MANAGER', 'SYSTEM_ADMIN'].includes(userRole)
  const isAuthenticated = Boolean(localStorage.getItem('token'))
  const ThemeIcon = theme === 'dark' ? Moon : theme === 'light' ? Sun : Monitor
  const handleOpenSettings = () => {
    onNavigate?.()
    openSettingsModal(isAuthenticated)
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col bg-sidebar text-sidebar-foreground">
      {/* Logo */}
      <div className="flex h-14 items-center border-b border-sidebar-border px-4 shrink-0">
        <Link to="/" className="flex items-center no-underline font-display font-bold text-2xl tracking-tight select-none">
          <span className="text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50">TRU</span>
          <span className="text-primary">SS</span>
        </Link>
      </div>
      {/* Nav */}
      <ScrollArea className="flex-1 py-3">
        <nav className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 px-2">
          <NavLink icon={LayoutDashboard} label="Dashboard"    href="/dashboard" exact />
          <NavLink icon={Car}             label="Araçlar"      href="/vehicles" />
          <NavLink icon={Fuel}      label="Yakıt"        href="/yakitlar" />
          <NavLink icon={Wrench}    label="Bakım"        href="/bakim" />
          <NavLink icon={Cog}       label="Parça Takibi" href="/parcalar" />
          {canAccessManagement && (
            <>
              <NavLink icon={BarChart3} label="Raporlar"      href="/raporlar" />
              <NavLink icon={Users}     label="Ekip Yönetimi" href="/ekip" />
            </>
          )}
        </nav>
      </ScrollArea>
      {/* Bottom */}
      <div className="border-t border-sidebar-border p-2 flex flex-col gap-0.5 shrink-0">
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className={bottomBtnBase}>
              <ThemeIcon className="size-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>Tema</span>
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent side="right" align="end">
            <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => setTheme('light')}>
              <Sun className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Açık
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => setTheme('dark')}>
              <Moon className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Koyu
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => setTheme('system')}>
              <Monitor className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Sistem
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={handleOpenSettings}
          aria-haspopup="dialog"
          aria-expanded={isSettingsOpen}
          className={cn(
            bottomBtnBase,
            isSettingsOpen && 'text-foreground bg-accent/50'
          )}
        >
          <Settings className="size-4 shrink-0" />
          <span>Ayarlar</span>
        </Button>
        {isAuthenticated && (
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => handleLogout(navigate)}
            className="w-full justify-start gap-3 text-destructive/70 hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
          >
            <LogOut className="size-4 shrink-0" />
            <span>Çıkış Yap</span>
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
export function MobileSidebar() {
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false)
  return (
    <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <SheetTrigger asChild>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="md:hidden">
          <Menu className="h-5 w-5" />
          <span className="sr-only">Menüyü aç</span>
        </Button>
      </SheetTrigger>
      <SheetContent side="left" className="w-60 p-0 bg-sidebar border-sidebar-border" showCloseButton={false}>
        <SheetHeader className="sr-only">
          <SheetTitle>Navigasyon</SheetTitle>
        </SheetHeader>
        <SidebarContent onNavigate={() => setOpen(false)} />
      </SheetContent>
    </Sheet>
  )
}
export default function Sidebar() {
  return (
    <aside className="hidden md:flex md:flex-col md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:left-0 md:w-60 md:z-30 border-r border-sidebar-border">
      <SidebarContent />
    </aside>
  )
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/ui/alert-dialog.jsx

````javascript
"use client"
import * as React from "react"
import { AlertDialog as AlertDialogPrimitive } from "radix-ui"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
function AlertDialog({
  ...props
}) {
  return <AlertDialogPrimitive.Root data-slot="alert-dialog" {...props} />;
}
function AlertDialogTrigger({
  ...props
}) {
  return (<AlertDialogPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="alert-dialog-trigger" {...props} />);
}
function AlertDialogPortal({
  ...props
}) {
  return (<AlertDialogPrimitive.Portal data-slot="alert-dialog-portal" {...props} />);
}
function AlertDialogOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay
      data-slot="alert-dialog-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/30 duration-100 backdrop-blur-sm data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function AlertDialogContent({
  className,
  size = "default",
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPortal>
      <AlertDialogOverlay />
      <AlertDialogPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="alert-dialog-content"
        data-size={size}
        className={cn(
          "group fixed top-1/2 left-1/2 z-50 grid w-full max-w-md -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 gap-6 rounded-xl bg-popover p-6 text-popover-foreground shadow-xl ring-1 ring-foreground/5 duration-100 outline-none dark:ring-foreground/10 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95 data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95",
          className
        )}
        {...props} />
    </AlertDialogPortal>
  );
}
function AlertDialogHeader({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-dialog-header"
      className={cn(
        "grid grid-rows-[auto_1fr] place-items-center gap-1.5 text-center has-data-[slot=alert-dialog-media]:grid-rows-[auto_auto_1fr] has-data-[slot=alert-dialog-media]:gap-x-6 sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:place-items-start sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:text-left sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:has-data-[slot=alert-dialog-media]:grid-rows-[auto_1fr]",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function AlertDialogFooter({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-dialog-footer"
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 group-data-[size=sm]/alert-dialog-content:grid group-data-[size=sm]/alert-dialog-content:grid-cols-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function AlertDialogMedia({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-dialog-media"
      className={cn(
        "mb-2 inline-flex size-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:row-span-2 *:[svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-8",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function AlertDialogTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="alert-dialog-title"
      className={cn(
        "font-heading text-lg font-medium sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:group-has-data-[slot=alert-dialog-media]/alert-dialog-content:col-start-2",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function AlertDialogDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Description
      data-slot="alert-dialog-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-sm text-balance text-muted-foreground md:text-pretty *:[a]:underline *:[a]:underline-offset-3 *:[a]:hover:text-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function AlertDialogAction({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <Button variant={variant} size={size} asChild>
      <AlertDialogPrimitive.Action data-slot="alert-dialog-action" className={cn(className)} {...props} />
    </Button>
  );
}
function AlertDialogCancel({
  className,
  variant = "outline",
  size = "default",
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <Button variant={variant} size={size} asChild>
      <AlertDialogPrimitive.Cancel data-slot="alert-dialog-cancel" className={cn(className)} {...props} />
    </Button>
  );
}
export {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogMedia,
  AlertDialogOverlay,
  AlertDialogPortal,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/ui/alert.jsx
````javascript
import * as React from "react"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
const alertVariants = cva(
  "relative w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm flex gap-3 items-start [&>svg]:size-4 [&>svg]:mt-0.5 [&>svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:     "bg-card text-foreground border-border",
        destructive: "border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 text-destructive [&>svg]:text-destructive",
        warning:     "border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 [&>svg]:text-amber-500",
        info:        "border-primary/40 bg-primary/10 text-primary [&>svg]:text-primary",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "default" },
  }
)
function Alert({ className, variant, ...props }) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert"
      role="alert"
      className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}
function AlertTitle({ className, ...props }) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-title"
      className={cn("font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}
function AlertDescription({ className, ...props }) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-description"
      className={cn("text-sm [&_p]:leading-relaxed opacity-90", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}
export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription }
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/ui/badge.jsx
````javascript
import * as React from "react"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority";
import { Slot } from "radix-ui"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
const badgeVariants = cva(
  "group/badge inline-flex h-5 w-fit shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-transparent px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&>svg]:pointer-events-none [&>svg]:size-3!",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-primary/80",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-secondary/80",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40 [a]:hover:bg-destructive/20",
        outline:
          "border-border text-foreground [a]:hover:bg-muted [a]:hover:text-muted-foreground",
        ghost:
          "hover:bg-muted hover:text-muted-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)
function Badge({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : "span"
  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="badge"
      data-variant={variant}
      className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props} />
  );
}
export { Badge, badgeVariants }
````

````javascript
import * as React from "react"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority";
import { Slot } from "radix-ui"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-transparent bg-clip-padding text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all outline-none select-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring focus-visible:ring-ring/30 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:border-destructive/50 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/80",
        outline:
          "border-zinc-700/80 bg-zinc-900/80 text-zinc-200 hover:bg-primary/15 hover:border-primary/50 hover:text-white",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklch,var(--secondary),var(--foreground)_5%)] aria-expanded:bg-secondary aria-expanded:text-secondary-foreground",
        ghost:
          "hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/20 focus-visible:border-destructive/40 focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:hover:bg-destructive/30 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default:
          "h-9 px-3",
        xs: "h-6 px-2.5 text-xs [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        sm: "h-8 px-3",
        lg: "h-10 px-4",
        icon: "size-9",
        "icon-xs": "size-6 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        "icon-sm": "size-8",
        "icon-lg": "size-10",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)
function Button({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : "button"
  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="button"
      data-variant={variant}
      data-size={size}
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      {...props} />
  );
}
export { Button, buttonVariants }
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/ui/chart.jsx
````javascript
import * as React from "react"
import * as RechartsPrimitive from "recharts"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
// Format: { THEME_NAME: CSS_SELECTOR }
const THEMES = {
  light: "",
  dark: ".dark"
}
const INITIAL_DIMENSION = {
  width: 320,
  height: 200
}
const ChartContext = React.createContext(null)
function useChart() {
  const context = React.useContext(ChartContext)
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useChart must be used within a <ChartContainer />")
  }
  return context
}
function ChartContainer({
  id,
  className,
  children,
  config,
  initialDimension = INITIAL_DIMENSION,
  ...props
}) {
  const uniqueId = React.useId()
  const chartId = `chart-${id ?? uniqueId.replace(/:/g, "")}`
  return (
    <ChartContext.Provider value={{ config }}>
      <div
        data-slot="chart"
        data-chart={chartId}
        className={cn(
          "flex aspect-video justify-center text-xs [&_.recharts-cartesian-axis-tick_text]:fill-muted-foreground [&_.recharts-cartesian-grid_line[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border/50 [&_.recharts-curve.recharts-tooltip-cursor]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-dot[stroke='#fff']]:stroke-transparent [&_.recharts-layer]:outline-hidden [&_.recharts-polar-grid_[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-radial-bar-background-sector]:fill-muted [&_.recharts-rectangle.recharts-tooltip-cursor]:fill-muted [&_.recharts-reference-line_[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-sector]:outline-hidden [&_.recharts-sector[stroke='#fff']]:stroke-transparent [&_.recharts-surface]:outline-hidden",
          className
        )}
        {...props}>
        <ChartStyle id={chartId} config={config} />
        <RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer initialDimension={initialDimension}>
          {children}
        </RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </ChartContext.Provider>
  );
}
const ChartStyle = ({
  id,
  config
}) => {
  const safeConfig = (config !== null && typeof config === 'object') ? config : {}
  const colorConfig = Object.entries(safeConfig).filter(([, config]) => config.theme ?? config.color)
  if (!colorConfig.length) {
    return null
  }
  return (
    <style
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
        __html: Object.entries(THEMES)
          .map(([theme, prefix]) => `
${prefix} [data-chart=${id}] {
${colorConfig
.map(([key, itemConfig]) => {
const color =
  itemConfig.theme?.[theme] ??
  itemConfig.color
return color ? `  --color-${key}: ${color};` : null
})
.join("\n")}
}
`)
          .join("\n"),
      }} />
  );
}
const ChartTooltip = RechartsPrimitive.Tooltip
function ChartTooltipContent({
  active,
  payload,
  className,
  indicator = "dot",
  hideLabel = false,
  hideIndicator = false,
  label,
  labelFormatter,
  labelClassName,
  formatter,
  color,
  nameKey,
  labelKey
}) {
  const { config } = useChart()
  const tooltipLabel = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (hideLabel || !payload?.length) {
      return null
    }
    const [item] = payload
    const key = `${labelKey ?? item?.dataKey ?? item?.name ?? "value"}`
    const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
    const value =
      !labelKey && typeof label === "string"
        ? (config[label]?.label ?? label)
        : itemConfig?.label
    if (labelFormatter) {
      return (
        <div className={cn("font-medium", labelClassName)}>
          {labelFormatter(value, payload)}
        </div>
      );
    }
    if (!value) {
      return null
    }
    return <div className={cn("font-medium", labelClassName)}>{value}</div>;
  }, [
    label,
    labelFormatter,
    payload,
    hideLabel,
    labelClassName,
    config,
    labelKey,
  ])
  if (!active || !payload?.length) {
    return null
  }
  const nestLabel = payload.length === 1 && indicator !== "dot"
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "grid min-w-32 items-start gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-popover px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-popover-foreground shadow-lg ring-1 ring-foreground/5 dark:ring-foreground/10",
        className
      )}>
      {!nestLabel ? tooltipLabel : null}
      <div className="grid gap-1.5">
        {payload
          .filter((item) => item.type !== "none")
          .map((item, index) => {
            const key = `${nameKey ?? item.name ?? item.dataKey ?? "value"}`
            const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
            const indicatorColor = color ?? item.payload?.fill ?? item.color
            return (
              <div
                key={index}
                className={cn(
                  "flex w-full flex-wrap items-stretch gap-2 [&>svg]:h-2.5 [&>svg]:w-2.5 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground",
                  indicator === "dot" && "items-center"
                )}>
                {formatter && item?.value !== undefined && item.name ? (
                  formatter(item.value, item.name, item, index, item.payload)
                ) : (
                  <>
                    {itemConfig?.icon ? (
                      <itemConfig.icon />
                    ) : (
                      !hideIndicator && (
                        <div
                          className={cn("shrink-0 rounded-[2px] border-[var(--color-border)] bg-[var(--color-bg)]", {
                            "h-2.5 w-2.5": indicator === "dot",
                            "w-1": indicator === "line",
                            "w-0 border-[1.5px] border-dashed bg-transparent":
                              indicator === "dashed",
                            "my-0.5": nestLabel && indicator === "dashed",
                          })}
                          style={
                            {
                              "--color-bg": indicatorColor,
                              "--color-border": indicatorColor
                            }
                          } />
                      )
                    )}
                    <div
                      className={cn(
                        "flex flex-1 justify-between leading-none",
                        nestLabel ? "items-end" : "items-center"
                      )}>
                      <div className="grid gap-1.5">
                        {nestLabel ? tooltipLabel : null}
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                          {itemConfig?.label ?? item.name}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {item.value != null && (
                        <span className="font-mono font-medium text-foreground tabular-nums">
                          {typeof item.value === "number"
                            ? item.value.toLocaleString()
                            : String(item.value)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
const ChartLegend = RechartsPrimitive.Legend
function ChartLegendContent({
  className,
  hideIcon = false,
  payload,
  verticalAlign = "bottom",
  nameKey
}) {
  const { config } = useChart()
  if (!payload?.length) {
    return null
  }
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center justify-center gap-4",
        verticalAlign === "top" ? "pb-3" : "pt-3",
        className
      )}>
      {payload
        .filter((item) => item.type !== "none")
        .map((item, index) => {
          const key = `${nameKey ?? item.dataKey ?? "value"}`
          const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
          return (
            <div
              key={index}
              className={cn(
                "flex items-center gap-1.5 [&>svg]:h-3 [&>svg]:w-3 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground"
              )}>
              {itemConfig?.icon && !hideIcon ? (
                <itemConfig.icon />
              ) : (
                <div
                  className="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-[2px]"
                  style={{
                    backgroundColor: item.color,
                  }} />
              )}
              {itemConfig?.label}
            </div>
          );
        })}
    </div>
  );
}
function getPayloadConfigFromPayload(
  config,
  payload,
  key
) {
  if (typeof payload !== "object" || payload === null) {
    return undefined
  }
  const payloadPayload =
    "payload" in payload &&
    typeof payload.payload === "object" &&
    payload.payload !== null
      ? payload.payload
      : undefined
  let configLabelKey = key
  if (
    key in payload &&
    typeof payload[key] === "string"
  ) {
    configLabelKey = payload[key]
  } else if (
    payloadPayload &&
    key in payloadPayload &&
    typeof payloadPayload[key] === "string"
  ) {
    configLabelKey = payloadPayload[key]
  }
  return configLabelKey in config ? config[configLabelKey] : config[key]
}
export {
  ChartContainer,
  ChartTooltip,
  ChartTooltipContent,
  ChartLegend,
  ChartLegendContent,
  ChartStyle,
}
// Re-export recharts primitives so pages import from here, not directly from recharts
export const {
  Area, AreaChart, Bar, BarChart, CartesianGrid, Cell,
  Label, Legend, Line, LineChart, Pie, PieChart,
  PolarAngleAxis, PolarGrid, PolarRadiusAxis,
  Radar, RadarChart, ReferenceLine, ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis,
} = RechartsPrimitive
````
