````javascript
import * as React from "react"
import { Dialog as DialogPrimitive } from "radix-ui"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { XIcon } from "lucide-react"
function Dialog({
  ...props
}) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Root data-slot="dialog" {...props} />;
}
function DialogTrigger({
  ...props
}) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="dialog-trigger" {...props} />;
}
function DialogPortal({
  ...props
}) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Portal data-slot="dialog-portal" {...props} />;
}
function DialogClose({
  ...props
}) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Close data-slot="dialog-close" {...props} />;
}
function DialogOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Overlay
      data-slot="dialog-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-0 isolate z-50 bg-black/30 duration-100 backdrop-blur-sm data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function DialogContent({
  className,
  children,
  showCloseButton = true,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <DialogPortal>
      <DialogOverlay />
      <DialogPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="dialog-content"
        className={cn(
          "fixed top-1/2 left-1/2 z-50 grid w-full max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 gap-6 rounded-xl bg-popover p-6 text-sm text-popover-foreground shadow-xl ring-1 ring-foreground/5 duration-100 outline-none sm:max-w-lg dark:ring-foreground/10 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95 data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95",
          className
        )}
        {...props}>
        {children}
        {showCloseButton && (
          <DialogPrimitive.Close data-slot="dialog-close" asChild>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-secondary"
              size="icon-sm">
              <XIcon />
              <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
            </Button>
          </DialogPrimitive.Close>
        )}
      </DialogPrimitive.Content>
    </DialogPortal>
  );
}
function DialogHeader({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-header"
      className={cn("flex flex-col gap-1.5", className)}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function DialogFooter({
  className,
  showCloseButton = false,
  children,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-footer"
      className={cn("flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end", className)}
      {...props}>
      {children}
      {showCloseButton && (
        <DialogPrimitive.Close asChild>
          <Button variant="outline">Close</Button>
        </DialogPrimitive.Close>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
function DialogTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="dialog-title"
      className={cn("font-heading text-base leading-none font-medium", className)}
      {...props} />
  );
}
function DialogDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Description
      data-slot="dialog-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-sm text-muted-foreground *:[a]:underline *:[a]:underline-offset-3 *:[a]:hover:text-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  );
}
export {
  Dialog,
  DialogClose,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogOverlay,
  DialogPortal,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/theme-provider.js
````javascript
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
const ThemeProviderContext = createContext(undefined);
export function ThemeProvider({ children, defaultTheme = 'system', storageKey = 'vite-ui-theme' }) {
    const [theme, setThemeState] = useState(
        () => localStorage.getItem(storageKey) || defaultTheme
    );
    useEffect(() => {
        const root = window.document.documentElement;
        root.classList.remove('light', 'dark');
        const resolved =
            theme === 'system'
                ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
                    ? 'dark'
                    : 'light'
                : theme;
        root.classList.add(resolved);
        root.setAttribute('data-theme', resolved);
    }, [theme]);
    const setTheme = (newTheme) => {
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, newTheme);
        setThemeState(newTheme);
    };
    const toggleTheme = () => setTheme(theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark');
    return (
        <ThemeProviderContext.Provider value={{ theme, setTheme, toggleTheme }}>
            {children}
        </ThemeProviderContext.Provider>
    );
}
export const useTheme = () => {
    const context = useContext(ThemeProviderContext);
    if (context === undefined) throw new Error('useTheme must be used within a ThemeProvider');
    return context;
};
````

````javascript
      {/* ── Vizyon / Misyon / Güven ── */}
      <section className="border-t border-border/50 py-20">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="mb-12 text-center">
            {loading ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
                <Skeleton className="h-9 w-40 rounded-xl" />
                <Skeleton className="h-5 w-72 rounded-lg" />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl">Neden TRUSS?</h2>
                <p className="mt-3 text-muted-foreground">Misyonumuz, vizyonumuz ve değerlerimiz.</p>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">
            {loading
              ? Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
                  <Card key={i} className="p-6">
                    <Skeleton className="mb-4 h-10 w-10 rounded-xl" />
                    <Skeleton className="mb-3 h-6 w-28 rounded-md" />
                    <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full rounded-md mb-2" />
                    <Skeleton className="h-4 w-5/6 rounded-md mb-2" />
                    <Skeleton className="h-4 w-4/6 rounded-md" />
                  </Card>
                ))
              : visionCards.map(({ icon: Icon, title, description }) => (
                  <Card
                    key={title}
                    className="transition-all duration-240 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg"
                  >
                    <CardHeader>
                      <div className="mb-2 inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary/10 text-primary">
                        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <CardTitle className="text-lg">{title}</CardTitle>
                    </CardHeader>
                    <CardContent>
                      <CardDescription className="text-sm leading-relaxed">{description}</CardDescription>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
      {/* ── Özellikler ── */}
      <section className="border-t border-border/50 py-20 bg-muted/30">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="mb-12 text-center">
            {loading ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
                <Skeleton className="h-9 w-52 rounded-xl" />
                <Skeleton className="h-5 w-64 rounded-lg" />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl">Temel Özellikler</h2>
                <p className="mt-3 text-muted-foreground">Filonuzu yönetmek için ihtiyacınız olan her şey.</p>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">
            {loading
              ? Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
                  <Card key={i} className="p-6">
                    <Skeleton className="mb-4 h-10 w-10 rounded-xl" />
                    <Skeleton className="mb-3 h-6 w-36 rounded-md" />
                    <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full rounded-md mb-2" />
                    <Skeleton className="h-4 w-5/6 rounded-md" />
                  </Card>
                ))
              : featureCards.map(({ icon: Icon, title, description }) => (
                  <Card
                    key={title}
                    className="transition-all duration-240 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg"
                  >
                    <CardHeader>
                      <div className="mb-2 inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary/10 text-primary">
                        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <CardTitle className="text-lg">{title}</CardTitle>
                    </CardHeader>
                    <CardContent>
                      <CardDescription className="text-sm leading-relaxed">{description}</CardDescription>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
      {/* ── CTA ── */}
      {!loading && (
        <section className="border-t border-border/50 py-20">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 text-center sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl">
              Filonuzu bugün dijitalleştirin
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-4 text-muted-foreground">
              Ücretsiz hesap oluşturun, ilk aracınızı ekleyin ve farkı görün.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center">
              <Link to="/register">
                <Button size="lg" className="w-full sm:w-auto">
                  Hemen Başla
                  <ArrowRight className="ml-2 h-4 w-4" />
                </Button>
              </Link>
              <Link to="/login">
                <Button size="lg" variant="outline" className="w-full sm:w-auto">
                  Zaten hesabım var
                </Button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
      {/* ── Footer ── */}
      <footer className="border-t border-border/50 py-10">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          © {new Date().getFullYear()} TRUSS Inc. · Filo Yönetim Platformu
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  )
}
````

````javascript
import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import toast from 'react-hot-toast'
import { Save, Trash2, Bell, Globe, ShieldAlert, User } from 'lucide-react'
import api from '@/services/api'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Checkbox } from '@/components/ui/checkbox'
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select'
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/alert-dialog'
const defaultPrefs = {
  dil: 'tr',
  para_birimi: 'TRY',
  olcu_birimi: 'km',
}
const defaultNotifs = {
  email_bildirim_aktif: true,
  sms_bildirim_aktif: false,
  bakim_uyarisi: true,
  evrak_uyarisi: true,
}
export default function Settings() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState(defaultPrefs)
  const [notifs, setNotifs] = useState(defaultNotifs)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [savingPrefs, setSavingPrefs] = useState(false)
  const [savingNotifs, setSavingNotifs] = useState(false)
  const fetchSettings = useCallback(async () => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token')
    if (!token) { navigate('/login'); return }
    try {
      setLoading(true)
      const [pRes, nRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
        api.get('/users/settings/preferences'),
        api.get('/users/settings/notifications'),
      ])
      if (pRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
        setPrefs(prev => ({ ...prev, ...pRes.value.data }))
      }
      if (nRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
        setNotifs(prev => ({ ...prev, ...nRes.value.data }))
      }
    } catch {
      // silently ignore — defaults remain
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [navigate])
  useEffect(() => { fetchSettings() }, [fetchSettings])
  const handleSavePrefs = async () => {
    try {
      setSavingPrefs(true)
      await api.put('/users/settings/preferences', prefs)
      toast.success('Tercihler kaydedildi.')
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Tercihler kaydedilemedi.')
    } finally {
      setSavingPrefs(false)
    }
  }
  const handleSaveNotifs = async () => {
    try {
      setSavingNotifs(true)
      await api.put('/users/settings/notifications', notifs)
      toast.success('Bildirim ayarları kaydedildi.')
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Bildirim ayarları kaydedilemedi.')
    } finally {
      setSavingNotifs(false)
    }
  }
  const handleDeleteAccount = async () => {
    try {
      await api.delete('/users/me')
      localStorage.removeItem('token')
      navigate('/')
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Hesap silinemedi.')
    }
  }
  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-48 text-muted-foreground text-sm">
        Ayarlar yükleniyor…
      </div>
    )
  }
  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Ayarlar</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">Hesap ve uygulama tercihlerinizi yönetin.</p>
      </div>
      {/* Genel Tercihler */}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader className="pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Globe className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
            <CardTitle className="text-base">Genel Tercihler</CardTitle>
          </div>
          <CardDescription>Dil, para birimi ve ölçü birimi ayarları.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="dil">Dil</Label>
              <Select value={prefs.dil} onValueChange={v => setPrefs(p => ({ ...p, dil: v }))}>
                <SelectTrigger id="dil">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="tr">Türkçe</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="en">English</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="para_birimi">Para Birimi</Label>
              <Select value={prefs.para_birimi} onValueChange={v => setPrefs(p => ({ ...p, para_birimi: v }))}>
                <SelectTrigger id="para_birimi">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="TRY">₺ Türk Lirası</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="USD">$ ABD Doları</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="EUR">€ Euro</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="olcu_birimi">Ölçü Birimi</Label>
              <Select value={prefs.olcu_birimi} onValueChange={v => setPrefs(p => ({ ...p, olcu_birimi: v }))}>
                <SelectTrigger id="olcu_birimi">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="km">Kilometre (km)</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="mi">Mil (mi)</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
            <Button size="sm" className="gap-2" onClick={handleSavePrefs} disabled={savingPrefs}>
              <Save className="size-4" />
              {savingPrefs ? 'Kaydediliyor…' : 'Kaydet'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      {/* Bildirim Ayarları */}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader className="pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Bell className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
            <CardTitle className="text-base">Bildirim Ayarları</CardTitle>
          </div>
          <CardDescription>Hangi kanallardan ve konulardan bildirim alacağınızı seçin.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-5">
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">Kanallar</p>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="email-switch" className="text-sm font-medium">E-posta Bildirimleri</Label>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Önemli güncellemeleri e-posta ile al.</p>
              </div>
              <Switch
                id="email-switch"
                checked={!!notifs.email_bildirim_aktif}
                onCheckedChange={v => setNotifs(p => ({ ...p, email_bildirim_aktif: v }))}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="sms-switch" className="text-sm font-medium">SMS Bildirimleri</Label>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Acil durumlarda SMS al.</p>
              </div>
              <Switch
                id="sms-switch"
                checked={!!notifs.sms_bildirim_aktif}
                onCheckedChange={v => setNotifs(p => ({ ...p, sms_bildirim_aktif: v }))}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3 border-t border-border pt-4">
            <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">Uyarı Türleri</p>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Checkbox
                id="bakim-uyarisi"
                checked={!!notifs.bakim_uyarisi}
                onCheckedChange={v => setNotifs(p => ({ ...p, bakim_uyarisi: !!v }))}
              />
              <Label htmlFor="bakim-uyarisi" className="text-sm cursor-pointer">Bakım zamanı uyarıları</Label>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Checkbox
                id="evrak-uyarisi"
                checked={!!notifs.evrak_uyarisi}
                onCheckedChange={v => setNotifs(p => ({ ...p, evrak_uyarisi: !!v }))}
              />
              <Label htmlFor="evrak-uyarisi" className="text-sm cursor-pointer">Evrak süresi uyarıları (muayene, kasko, sigorta)</Label>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
            <Button size="sm" className="gap-2" onClick={handleSaveNotifs} disabled={savingNotifs}>
              <Save className="size-4" />
              {savingNotifs ? 'Kaydediliyor…' : 'Kaydet'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      {/* Güvenlik */}
      <Card className="border-destructive/30">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShieldAlert className="size-4 text-destructive" />
            <CardTitle className="text-base text-destructive">Tehlikeli Bölge</CardTitle>
          </div>
          <CardDescription>Bu işlemler geri alınamaz. Dikkatli olun.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">Hesabı Sil</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Tüm araç, bakım ve yakıt verileriniz kalıcı olarak silinir.</p>
            </div>
            <AlertDialog>
              <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                <Button variant="destructive" size="sm" className="gap-2">
                  <Trash2 className="size-4" />
                  Hesabı Sil
                </Button>
              </AlertDialogTrigger>
              <AlertDialogContent>
                <AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogTitle>Hesabınızı silmek istediğinizden emin misiniz?</AlertDialogTitle>
                  <AlertDialogDescription>
                    Bu işlem geri alınamaz. Hesabınız ve buna bağlı tüm veriler (araçlar, bakım kayıtları, yakıt
                    kayıtları) kalıcı olarak silinecektir.
                  </AlertDialogDescription>
                </AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogFooter>
                  <AlertDialogCancel>İptal</AlertDialogCancel>
                  <AlertDialogAction
                    onClick={handleDeleteAccount}
                    className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
                  >
                    Evet, hesabı sil
                  </AlertDialogAction>
````

## File: Frontend/src/pages/Vehicles.jsx
````javascript
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { Plus, Fuel, Download, ArrowUpDown, ArrowUp, ArrowDown, Eye, Pencil, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import yakitServiceModule from '@/services/yakitService';
import { formatLocaleNumber, parseLocaleNumber } from '@/utils/formatters';
import { exportToCSV } from '@/utils/exporter';
import { AddVehicleModal, QuickFuelModal } from '@/components/dashboard';
import useVehicleStore from '@/store/useVehicleStore';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import {
    AlertDialog,
    AlertDialogAction,
    AlertDialogCancel,
    AlertDialogContent,
    AlertDialogDescription,
    AlertDialogFooter,
    AlertDialogHeader,
    AlertDialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/alert-dialog';
import {
    Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow,
} from '@/components/ui/table';
function AracDurumBadge({ arac, riskliIdler }) {
    const tehlikede = riskliIdler.tehlike.includes(arac.arac_id);
    const evrakSorunu = riskliIdler.evrak.includes(arac.arac_id);
    if (tehlikede) return <Badge variant="destructive" className="text-xs">Bakım Gerekli</Badge>;
    if (evrakSorunu) return <Badge variant="outline" className="border-orange-400/50 text-orange-500 text-xs">Evrak Sorunu</Badge>;
    return <Badge variant="outline" className="border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-500 text-xs">Sorunsuz</Badge>;
}
export default function Vehicles() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const araclar = useVehicleStore((s) => s.araclar);
    const loading = useVehicleStore((s) => s.loading);
    const riskliAracIdler = useVehicleStore((s) => s.riskliAracIdler);
    const fetchAraclar = useVehicleStore((s) => s.fetchAraclar);
    const openModal = useVehicleStore((s) => s.openModal);
    const openEditModal = useVehicleStore((s) => s.openEditModal);
    const deleteVehicle = useVehicleStore((s) => s.deleteVehicle);
    const [aktifFiltre, setAktifFiltre] = useState(searchParams.get('filtre') || 'tum');
    const [aramaMetni, setAramaMetni] = useState('');
    const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState({ key: 'plaka', order: 'asc' });
    // Quick Fuel Modal
    const [isQuickFuelModalOpen, setIsQuickFuelModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isQuickFuelModalVisible, setIsQuickFuelModalVisible] = useState(false);
    const [quickFuelData, setQuickFuelData] = useState({ arac_id: '', tarih: '', litre: '', tutar: '', km: '' });
    const [quickFuelError, setQuickFuelError] = useState(null);
    const [deleteId, setDeleteId] = useState(null);
    const [isQuickFuelSubmitting, setIsQuickFuelSubmitting] = useState(false);
    const quickFuelAracSelectRef = useRef(null);
    const quickFuelCloseTimerRef = useRef(null);
    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
        if (!token) { navigate('/login'); return; }
        fetchAraclar();
    }, [navigate, fetchAraclar]);
    useEffect(() => () => {
        if (quickFuelCloseTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(quickFuelCloseTimerRef.current);
    }, []);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isQuickFuelModalOpen && isQuickFuelModalVisible && quickFuelAracSelectRef.current) quickFuelAracSelectRef.current.focus();
    }, [isQuickFuelModalOpen, isQuickFuelModalVisible]);
    // Filtered + sorted vehicle list
    const gosterilenAraclar = React.useMemo(() => {
        let liste = araclar;
        if (aktifFiltre === 'tehlike') {
            const ids = new Set(riskliAracIdler.tehlike);
            liste = araclar.filter(a => ids.has(a.arac_id));
        } else if (aktifFiltre === 'evrak') {
            const ids = new Set(riskliAracIdler.evrak);
            liste = araclar.filter(a => ids.has(a.arac_id));
        }
        if (aramaMetni.trim()) {
            const q = aramaMetni.trim().toLowerCase();
            liste = liste.filter(a =>
                (a.plaka || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                (a.marka || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                (a.model || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
            );
        }
        return [...liste].sort((a, b) => {
            const av = String(a[sortConfig.key] ?? '').toLowerCase();
            const bv = String(b[sortConfig.key] ?? '').toLowerCase();
            return sortConfig.order === 'asc' ? av.localeCompare(bv) : bv.localeCompare(av);
        });
    }, [araclar, aktifFiltre, aramaMetni, sortConfig, riskliAracIdler]);
    const handleSort = (key) => setSortConfig(prev => ({
        key,
        order: prev.key === key && prev.order === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc',
    }));
    const SortIcon = ({ colKey }) => {
        if (sortConfig.key !== colKey) return <ArrowUpDown className="ml-1 inline-block size-3 opacity-40" />;
        return sortConfig.order === 'asc'
            ? <ArrowUp className="ml-1 inline-block size-3" />
            : <ArrowDown className="ml-1 inline-block size-3" />;
    };
    const handleFormEnterKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key !== 'Enter' || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return;
        e.preventDefault(); e.currentTarget.requestSubmit();
    };
    const handleDeleteConfirm = async () => {
        if (deleteId !== null) {
            await deleteVehicle(deleteId);
        }
        setDeleteId(null);
    };
    const openQuickFuelModal = () => {
        setQuickFuelError(null);
        setQuickFuelData({ arac_id: '', tarih: '', litre: '', tutar: '', km: '' });
        setIsQuickFuelModalOpen(true); setIsQuickFuelModalVisible(true);
    };
    const closeQuickFuelModal = () => {
        setIsQuickFuelModalVisible(false);
        quickFuelCloseTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { setIsQuickFuelModalOpen(false); setQuickFuelError(null); }, 200);
    };
    const handleQuickFuelInputChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setQuickFuelData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    };
    const handleQuickFuelNumberBlur = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        if (!['tutar', 'km'].includes(name)) return;
        const parsed = parseLocaleNumber(value);
        if (parsed === null) return;
        setQuickFuelData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: formatLocaleNumber(parsed, name === 'tutar' ? 2 : 0) }));
    };
    const handleQuickFuelSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault(); setQuickFuelError(null);
        const parsedLitre = parseLocaleNumber(quickFuelData.litre);
        const parsedTutar = parseLocaleNumber(quickFuelData.tutar);
        const parsedKm = parseLocaleNumber(quickFuelData.km);
        if (!quickFuelData.arac_id || !quickFuelData.tarih || parsedLitre === null || parsedTutar === null || parsedKm === null) {
            setQuickFuelError('Lütfen tüm alanları eksiksiz doldurun.'); return;
        }
        try {
            setIsQuickFuelSubmitting(true);
            await yakitServiceModule.yakitEkle({ arac_id: Number(quickFuelData.arac_id), yakit_tipi: 'Diğer', tarih: quickFuelData.tarih, litre: parsedLitre, tutar: parsedTutar, km: parsedKm });
            closeQuickFuelModal();
            toast.success('Yakıt kaydı eklendi.');
        } catch (err) {
            setQuickFuelError(err?.response?.data?.message || 'Yakıt eklenemedi.');
        } finally { setIsQuickFuelSubmitting(false); }
    };
    const filterBtns = [
        { key: 'tum', label: 'Tümü', count: araclar.length },
        { key: 'tehlike', label: 'Bakım Gerekli', count: riskliAracIdler.tehlike.length },
        { key: 'evrak', label: 'Evrak Sorunu', count: riskliAracIdler.evrak.length },
    ];
    return (
        <>
        <div className="space-y-4">
            {/* Kompakt Filtre ve Aksiyon Çubuğu (Toolbar) */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <div className="flex gap-1.5 flex-wrap items-center">
                    {filterBtns.map(({ key, label, count }) => (
                        <Button
                            key={key}
                            size="sm"
                            variant={aktifFiltre === key ? 'default' : 'outline'}
                            onClick={() => setAktifFiltre(key)}
                            className="h-8 gap-1.5 text-xs"
                        >
                            {label}
                            <span className={`rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold leading-none tabular-nums ${aktifFiltre === key ? 'bg-primary-foreground/20 text-primary-foreground' : 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground'}`}>
                                {count}
                            </span>
                        </Button>
                    ))}
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2">
                    <Input
                        type="search"
                        placeholder="Plaka, marka, model ara…"
                        className="h-8 w-full text-sm sm:w-52"
                        value={aramaMetni}
                        onChange={e => setAramaMetni(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="h-8 gap-1.5 text-xs" onClick={openQuickFuelModal}>
                        <Fuel className="size-3.5" /> Hızlı Yakıt
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                        size="sm"
                        variant="outline"
                        className="h-8 gap-1.5 text-xs"
                        onClick={() => {
                            const rows = gosterilenAraclar.map(a => ({ Plaka: a.plaka || '-', Marka: a.marka || '-', Model: a.model || '-', Yıl: a.yil || '-' }));
                            exportToCSV(rows, `araclar_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`);
                        }}
                    >
                        <Download className="size-3.5" /> CSV
                    </Button>
                    <Button size="sm" className="h-8 gap-1.5 text-xs" onClick={openModal}>
                        <Plus className="size-3.5" /> Araç Ekle
                    </Button>
                </div>
            </div>
            {/* Tablo */}
            {loading ? (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                    {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-muted/40 animate-pulse" />
                    ))}
                </div>
            ) : gosterilenAraclar.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-border p-3.5 py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground">
                    <p className="text-sm">Bu filtreye uyan araç bulunamadı.</p>
                    {aktifFiltre !== 'tum' && (
                        <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" className="mt-3 text-xs" onClick={() => setAktifFiltre('tum')}>
                            Tüm araçları göster
                        </Button>
                    )}
                </div>
            ) : (
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-border overflow-hidden">
                    <Table className="text-sm">
                        <TableHeader>
                            <TableRow className="bg-muted/30">
                                <TableHead className="w-32 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer select-none" onClick={() => handleSort('plaka')}>
                                    Plaka <SortIcon colKey="plaka" />
                                </TableHead>
                                <TableHead className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer select-none" onClick={() => handleSort('marka')}>
                                    Marka <SortIcon colKey="marka" />
                                </TableHead>
                                <TableHead className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer select-none" onClick={() => handleSort('model')}>
                                    Model <SortIcon colKey="model" />
                                </TableHead>
                                <TableHead className="w-20 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer select-none" onClick={() => handleSort('yil')}>
                                    Yıl <SortIcon colKey="yil" />
                                </TableHead>
                                <TableHead className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer select-none" onClick={() => handleSort('guncel_sayac')}>
                                    Sayaç <SortIcon colKey="guncel_sayac" />
                                </TableHead>
                                <TableHead className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium">Durum</TableHead>
                                <TableHead className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-right">İşlemler</TableHead>
                            </TableRow>
                        </TableHeader>
                        <TableBody>
                            {gosterilenAraclar.map(arac => {
                                const isSmh = arac.sayac_tipi === 'SMH' || Number(arac.calisma_saati_bakim) > 0;
                                const sayac = Number(arac.guncel_sayac ?? (isSmh ? arac.guncel_calisma_saati : arac.guncel_km) ?? 0);
                                return (
                                    <TableRow
                                        key={arac.arac_id}
                                        className="cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted/30"
                                        onClick={() => navigate(`/arac/${arac.arac_id}`)}
                                    >
                                        <TableCell className="px-3 py-2.5 font-mono font-semibold text-sm tabular-nums">{arac.plaka || '-'}</TableCell>
                                        <TableCell className="px-3 py-2.5 text-sm">{arac.marka || '-'}</TableCell>
                                        <TableCell className="px-3 py-2.5 text-sm">{arac.model || '-'}</TableCell>
                                        <TableCell className="px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{arac.yil || '-'}</TableCell>
                                        <TableCell className="px-3 py-2.5 text-sm tabular-nums">
                                            {sayac.toLocaleString('tr-TR')} {isSmh ? 'SMH' : 'KM'}
                                        </TableCell>
                                        <TableCell className="px-3 py-2.5 text-sm" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
                                            <AracDurumBadge arac={arac} riskliIdler={riskliAracIdler} />
                                        </TableCell>
                                        <TableCell className="px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-right" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
                                            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                                                <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" className="size-7" onClick={() => navigate(`/arac/${arac.arac_id}`)}>
                                                    <Eye className="size-3.5" />
                                                </Button>
                                                <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" className="size-7" onClick={() => openEditModal(arac)}>
                                                    <Pencil className="size-3.5" />
                                                </Button>
                                                <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" className="size-7 text-destructive hover:text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10" onClick={() => setDeleteId(arac.arac_id)}>
                                                    <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                                                </Button>
                                            </div>
                                        </TableCell>
                                    </TableRow>
                                );
                            })}
                        </TableBody>
                    </Table>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
        <AlertDialog open={deleteId !== null} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setDeleteId(null)}>
            <AlertDialogContent>
                <AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogTitle>Aracı sil?</AlertDialogTitle>
                    <AlertDialogDescription>
                        Bu araç ve ilişkili kayıtları silinecek. Bu işlem geri alınamaz.
                    </AlertDialogDescription>
                </AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogFooter>
                    <AlertDialogCancel>Vazgeç</AlertDialogCancel>
                    <AlertDialogAction variant="destructive" onClick={handleDeleteConfirm}>
                        Sil
                    </AlertDialogAction>
                </AlertDialogFooter>
            </AlertDialogContent>
        </AlertDialog>
        <AddVehicleModal />
        <QuickFuelModal
            isQuickFuelModalOpen={isQuickFuelModalOpen}
            isQuickFuelModalVisible={isQuickFuelModalVisible}
            quickFuelError={quickFuelError}
            handleQuickFuelSubmit={handleQuickFuelSubmit}
            handleFormEnterKeyDown={handleFormEnterKeyDown}
            quickFuelData={quickFuelData}
            handleQuickFuelInputChange={handleQuickFuelInputChange}
            handleQuickFuelNumberBlur={handleQuickFuelNumberBlur}
            araclar={araclar}
            closeQuickFuelModal={closeQuickFuelModal}
            isQuickFuelSubmitting={isQuickFuelSubmitting}
            quickFuelAracSelectRef={quickFuelAracSelectRef}
        />
        </>
    );
}
````

## File: Frontend/src/services/api.js
````javascript
import axios from 'axios';
const api = axios.create({
    baseURL: 'http://localhost:5001/api', // Backend'in ana kapısı
});
// 1. İSTEK (REQUEST) MÜFETTİŞİ: Giden her isteğe Bearer token ekler.
// Not: useAuthStore token'ı localStorage ile senkron tutar; store'u import etmek döngüsel bağımlılık yaratacağı için buradan okunur.
api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (token) {
        config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }
    return config;
});
const AUTH_ENDPOINTS = ['/users/login', '/users/register'];
// 2. CEVAP (RESPONSE) MÜFETTİŞİ: Gelen cevapları denetler.
api.interceptors.response.use(
    (response) => {
        // Her şey yolundaysa paketi içeri al.
        return response;
    },
    (error) => {
        const status = error.response?.status;
        const isAuthEndpoint = AUTH_ENDPOINTS.some((path) => error.config?.url?.includes(path));
        // Login/Register'daki 401 "hatalı şifre" demektir; yönlendirme yapılmaz, hata forma iletilir.
        if (!isAuthEndpoint && (status === 401 || status === 403)) {
            console.warn("🚨 Şantiye Güvenliği: Kimlik süresi doldu, dışarı atılıyorsunuz.");
            // Çürümüş oturumu temizle
            localStorage.removeItem('token');
            localStorage.removeItem('user');
            // React dışı bir dosyada olduğumuz için useNavigate kullanamayız, bu yüzden doğrudan window objesiyle yönlendiriyoruz.
            window.location.href = '/login';
        }
        // Hatayı sayfaya da ilet (formda uyarı göstermek istersek diye)
        return Promise.reject(error);
    }
);
export default api;
````

## File: Frontend/src/utils/exporter.js
````javascript
/**
 * Converts JSON array to CSV format with UTF-8 BOM for proper Turkish character handling
 * @param {Array} data - Array of objects to export
 * @param {string} filename - Output filename (without extension)
 * @param {Array} headers - Optional custom headers; if not provided, uses object keys
 */
export const exportToCSV = (data, filename = 'export', headers = null) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length === 0) {
        console.warn('exportToCSV: No data to export');
        return;
    }
    // Determine headers from data keys if not provided
    const columnHeaders = headers || Object.keys(data[0]);
    // Convert header row
    const headerRow = columnHeaders
        .map((h) => `"${String(h).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`)
        .join(',');
    // Convert data rows
    const dataRows = data.map((row) =>
        columnHeaders
            .map((header) => {
                const value = row[header];
                const strValue = String(value ?? '');
                // Escape quotes and wrap in quotes if contains comma, quote, or newline
                const escaped = strValue.replace(/"/g, '""');
                return /[,"\n]/.test(escaped) ? `"${escaped}"` : escaped;
            })
            .join(',')
    );
    // UTF-8 BOM for Excel/Turkish character support
    const bom = '\uFEFF';
    const csvContent = [headerRow, ...dataRows].join('\n');
    const fullContent = bom + csvContent;
    // Create Blob and trigger download
    const blob = new Blob([fullContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `${filename}.csv`;
    link.click();
    // Cleanup
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};
/**
 * Sorts an array of objects by a given key in ascending or descending order
 * @param {Array} data - Array of objects
 * @param {string} key - Property key to sort by
 * @param {string} order - 'asc' or 'desc'
 * @returns {Array} Sorted array
 */
export const sortByKey = (data, key, order = 'asc') => {
    if (!Array.isArray(data)) return data;
    const sorted = [...data].sort((a, b) => {
        let aVal = a[key];
        let bVal = b[key];
        // Handle null/undefined
        if (aVal == null && bVal == null) return 0;
        if (aVal == null) return order === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
        if (bVal == null) return order === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
        // Number comparison
        if (typeof aVal === 'number' && typeof bVal === 'number') {
            return order === 'asc' ? aVal - bVal : bVal - aVal;
        }
        // Date string comparison (ISO format or standard date)
        if (aVal instanceof Date || /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(String(aVal))) {
            const aDate = new Date(aVal);
            const bDate = new Date(bVal);
            if (!Number.isNaN(aDate.getTime()) && !Number.isNaN(bDate.getTime())) {
                return order === 'asc' ? aDate - bDate : bDate - aDate;
            }
        }
        // String comparison (case-insensitive)
        const aStr = String(aVal).toLowerCase();
        const bStr = String(bVal).toLowerCase();
        if (order === 'asc') {
            return aStr.localeCompare(bStr, 'tr-TR');
        } else {
            return bStr.localeCompare(aStr, 'tr-TR');
        }
    });
    return sorted;
};
````

## File: Frontend/src/utils/formatters.js
````javascript
export const parseLocaleNumber = (value) => {
    const normalized = String(value ?? '')
        .trim()
        .replace(/\s/g, '')
        .replace(/\./g, '')
        .replace(',', '.')
        .replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, '');
    if (!normalized) return null;
    const parsed = Number(normalized);
    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
};
export const formatLocaleNumber = (value, maximumFractionDigits = 0) => {
    const numericValue = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(numericValue)) return '';
    return new Intl.NumberFormat('tr-TR', {
        minimumFractionDigits: 0,
        maximumFractionDigits
    }).format(numericValue);
};
export const formatCurrency = (value, options = {}) => {
    const numericValue = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(numericValue)) return '-';
    const { locale = 'tr-TR', maximumFractionDigits = 0, suffix = 'TL' } = options;
    const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, {
        minimumFractionDigits: 0,
        maximumFractionDigits
    }).format(numericValue);
    return `${formatted} ${suffix}`;
};
export const formatTurkishPlate = (value) => {
    const normalized = String(value ?? '')
        .toLocaleUpperCase('tr-TR')
        .replace(/\s+/g, '')
        .replace(/[^0-9A-Z]/g, '');
    if (!normalized) return '';
    const cityMatch = normalized.match(/^\d{1,2}/);
    if (!cityMatch) {
        // Graceful fallback for non-standard entries without city code.
        return normalized.slice(0, 8);
    }
    const cityCode = cityMatch[0];
    const afterCity = normalized.slice(cityCode.length);
    const lettersMatch = afterCity.match(/^[A-Z]{1,3}/);
    const letters = lettersMatch ? lettersMatch[0] : '';
    const numbers = afterCity
        .slice(letters.length)
        .replace(/\D/g, '')
        .slice(0, 4);
    const segments = [cityCode];
    if (letters) segments.push(letters);
    if (numbers) segments.push(numbers);
    return segments.join(' ').trim();
};
````

## File: README.md
````markdown
# TRUSS — Filo Yönetim Sistemi

Araç filonuzu tek ekrandan yönetin. TRUSS; yakıt takibi, bakım planlaması ve gerçek zamanlı bildirimlerle kurumsal filo operasyonlarını kolaylaştıran modern bir web uygulamasıdır.

## Özellikler

- **Yönetim Merkezi** — Tüm araçların anlık durumu, yakıt tüketimi ve bakım özeti tek panelde
- **Araç Takibi** — Araç ekleme, düzenleme, yakıt girişi ve bakım geçmişi
- **Bakım Planlama** — Periyodik bakım hatırlatmaları ve servis takibi
- **Bildirim Sistemi** — E-posta ve SMS ile otomatik hatırlatmalar
- **Raporlama** — PDF çıktı desteğiyle yakıt ve bakım raporları
- **Karanlık Tema** — Shadcn tabanlı, tamamen özelleştirilebilir arayüz

## Teknoloji Yığını

| Katman | Teknoloji |
|---|---|
| Frontend | React 19, Vite, Tailwind CSS, Shadcn UI |
| State | Zustand |
| Routing | React Router v7 |
| Grafik | Recharts |
| Form | Zod |
| Backend | Node.js, Express |
| Veritabanı | PostgreSQL |
| Auth | JWT |
| Bildirim | Nodemailer (SMTP) |

## Kurulum

### Gereksinimler

- Node.js 18+
- PostgreSQL 14+

### Backend

```bash
cd Backend
cp .env.example .env
# .env dosyasını kendi bilgilerinizle düzenleyin
npm install
npm run dev
```

### Frontend

```bash
cd Frontend
npm install
npm run dev
```

Uygulama varsayılan olarak `http://localhost:5173` adresinde çalışır.

## Proje Yapısı

```
TRUSS/
├── Backend/
│   ├── routes/          # API rotaları
│   ├── services/        # E-posta, SMS, bildirim servisleri
│   ├── db.js            # Veritabanı bağlantısı
│   └── server.js        # Express sunucusu
└── Frontend/
    └── src/
        ├── components/  # Shadcn UI bileşenleri
        ├── pages/       # Sayfa bileşenleri
        └── store/       # Zustand store
```

## Lisans

MIT
````

````javascript
const express = require('express');
const authMiddleware = require('../middlewares/authMiddleware.js');
const { authorizeRoles } = require('../middlewares/rbacMiddleware.js');
const {
  getAllBakimlar,
  getBakimlarByArac,
  createBakim,
  updateBakim,
  deleteBakim,
} = require('../controllers/bakimController');
const router = express.Router();
router.get('/', authMiddleware, getAllBakimlar);
router.get('/arac/:arac_id', authMiddleware, getBakimlarByArac);
router.post('/', authMiddleware, createBakim);
router.put('/:bakim_id', authMiddleware, authorizeRoles('SYSTEM_ADMIN', 'FLEET_MANAGER'), updateBakim);
router.delete('/:bakim_id', authMiddleware, authorizeRoles('SYSTEM_ADMIN', 'FLEET_MANAGER'), deleteBakim);
module.exports = router;
````

````javascript
const nodemailer = require('nodemailer');
const smtpPort = parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT, 10) || 587;
const smtpSecure = String(process.env.SMTP_SECURE || 'false').toLowerCase() === 'true';
const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
  host: process.env.SMTP_HOST || 'smtp.gmail.com',
  port: smtpPort,
  secure: smtpSecure,
  auth: process.env.SMTP_USER && process.env.SMTP_PASS
    ? {
      user: process.env.SMTP_USER,
      pass: process.env.SMTP_PASS
    }
    : undefined

const buildObsidianTemplate = (subject, bodyHtml) => `
  <div style="margin:0;padding:0;background:#020617;font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;color:#e2e8f0;">
    <div style="max-width:640px;margin:0 auto;padding:32px 16px;">

        <div style="padding:24px 28px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(15,23,42,0.98),rgba(30,41,59,0.95));">

          ${bodyHtml}
        </div>
        <div style="padding:18px 28px;border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);font-size:12px;color:#64748b;">
          Bu mesaj TRUSS tarafından otomatik olarak gönderildi.
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
`;
const sendNotificationEmail = async (to, subject, htmlBody) => {
  if (!to) {
    throw new Error('E-posta alıcısı zorunludur.');
  }
  const from = process.env.SMTP_FROM || process.env.SMTP_USER || 'noreply@truss.local';
  const html = buildObsidianTemplate(subject || 'TRUSS Bildirimi', htmlBody || '<p>Bildirim içeriği yok.</p>');
  return transporter.sendMail({
    from,
    to,
    subject: subject || 'TRUSS Bildirimi',
    html
  });
};
module.exports = {
  sendNotificationEmail
};
````

## File: Backend/services/notificationService.js
````javascript
const nodemailer = require('nodemailer');
const smtpPort = parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT, 10) || 587;
const smtpSecure = String(process.env.SMTP_SECURE || 'false').toLowerCase() === 'true';
const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
  host: process.env.SMTP_HOST,
  port: smtpPort,
  secure: smtpSecure,
  auth: process.env.SMTP_USER && process.env.SMTP_PASS
    ? {
      user: process.env.SMTP_USER,
      pass: process.env.SMTP_PASS
    }
    : undefined
});
const sendEmail = async (userEmail, subject, text) => {
  if (!userEmail) return;
  const from = process.env.SMTP_FROM || process.env.SMTP_USER || 'noreply@truss.local';
  await transporter.sendMail({
    from,
    to: userEmail,
    subject,
    text
  });
};
const sendSMS = async (telefon, message) => {
  if (!telefon) return;
  // SMS saglayicisi entegre edilene kadar bildirimi log olarak birakir.
  console.log(`[SMS Taslak] ${telefon} -> ${message}`);
};
module.exports = {
  sendEmail,
  sendSMS
};
````

## File: Backend/services/smsService.js
````javascript
const green = '\u001b[32m';
const reset = '\u001b[0m';
const sendSMS = async (phoneNumber, message) => {
  if (!phoneNumber) {
    throw new Error('Telefon numarası zorunludur.');
  }
  const safeMessage = message || 'Bildirim içeriği yok.';
  console.log(`${green}[TRUSS SMS GATEWAY] -> Alıcı: ${phoneNumber} | Mesaj: ${safeMessage} | Durum: Başarılı${reset}`);
  return {
    success: true,
    phoneNumber,
    message: safeMessage
  };
};
module.exports = {
  sendSMS
};
````

````javascript
const path = require('path');
const { Pool } = require('pg'); // pg paketinden PostgreSQL bağlantı havuzunu oluşturmak için Pool sınıfını içe aktarır.
require('dotenv').config({ path: path.resolve(__dirname, '.env') }); // Backend/.env dosyasını çalışma dizininden bağımsız yükler.
const pool = new Pool({ // PostgreSQL bağlantı havuzunu oluşturur.
  user: process.env.DB_USER, // Veritabanı kullanıcı adını .env içinden alır.
  password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD, // Veritabanı şifresini .env içinden alır.
  host: process.env.DB_HOST, // Veritabanı sunucusunun adresini .env içinden alır.
  port: parseInt(process.env.DB_PORT, 10) || 5432, // Veritabanı portunu sayıya çevirir, yoksa varsayılan 5432 kullanır.
  database: process.env.DB_NAME, // Bağlanılacak veritabanı adını .env içinden alır.
}); // Pool yapılandırmasını tamamlar.
module.exports = pool; // Oluşturulan pool nesnesini diğer dosyalarda kullanılmak üzere dışa aktarır.
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/dashboard/AddVehicleModal.js
````javascript
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { TrussDatePicker } from '@/components/ui/truss-date-picker';
import useVehicleStore from '@/store/useVehicleStore';
import katalogService from '@/services/katalogService';
import { formatTurkishPlate } from '@/utils/formatters';
const AddVehicleModal = () => {
  const isModalOpen = useVehicleStore((s) => s.isModalOpen);
  const isModalVisible = useVehicleStore((s) => s.isModalVisible);
  const editingAracId = useVehicleStore((s) => s.editingAracId);
  const formData = useVehicleStore((s) => s.formData);
  const isSubmitting = useVehicleStore((s) => s.isSubmitting);
  const setFormField = useVehicleStore((s) => s.setFormField);
  const submitArac = useVehicleStore((s) => s.submitArac);
  const closeModal = useVehicleStore((s) => s.closeModal);
  const plakaInputRef = useRef(null);
  // 4 Kademeli Kaskad Katalog State'leri (Kategori -> Marka -> Model -> Varyant)
  const [kategoriler, setKategoriler] = useState([]);
  const [markalar, setMarkalar] = useState([]);
  const [modeller, setModeller] = useState([]);
  const [varyantlar, setVaryantlar] = useState([]);
  const [seciliKategoriId, setSeciliKategoriId] = useState('');
  const [seciliMarkaId, setSeciliMarkaId] = useState('');
  const [seciliModelId, setSeciliModelId] = useState('');
  const [seciliVaryantId, setSeciliVaryantId] = useState('');
  // Arka planda otomatik parça/bakım takibi için tutulan varyant bakım verileri
  const [varyantBakimBilgisi, setVaryantBakimBilgisi] = useState({
    onerilen_bakim_km: null,
    triger_degisim_km: null,
    calisma_saati_bakim: null,
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isModalOpen && isModalVisible) {
      plakaInputRef.current?.focus();
    }
  }, [isModalOpen, isModalVisible]);
  // Modal açıldığında kategorileri yükle
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isModalOpen) return;
    let active = true;
    katalogService
      .getKategoriler()
      .then((list) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setKategoriler(list);
        if (formData.kategori_id) {
          setSeciliKategoriId(String(formData.kategori_id));
        } else if (formData.kategori) {
          const eslesenKategori = list.find(
            (k) => k.ad.toLowerCase() === String(formData.kategori).toLowerCase()
          );
          if (eslesenKategori) {
            setSeciliKategoriId(String(eslesenKategori.kategori_id));
          } else {
            setSeciliKategoriId('');
          }
        } else {
          setSeciliKategoriId('');
        }
      })
      .catch(() => setKategoriler([]));
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [isModalOpen, formData.kategori_id, formData.kategori]);
  // Kategori seçildiğinde ilgili kategorinin markalarını yükle
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isModalOpen) return;
    if (!seciliKategoriId) {
      setMarkalar([]);
      setSeciliMarkaId('');
      setModeller([]);
      setSeciliModelId('');
      setVaryantlar([]);
      setSeciliVaryantId('');
      return;
    }
    let active = true;
    katalogService
      .getMarkalar(seciliKategoriId)
      .then((list) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setMarkalar(list);
        if (formData.marka) {
          const eslesenMarka = list.find(
            (m) => m.ad.toLowerCase() === String(formData.marka).toLowerCase()
          );
          if (eslesenMarka) {
            setSeciliMarkaId(String(eslesenMarka.marka_id));
          } else {
            setSeciliMarkaId('');
          }
        } else {
          setSeciliMarkaId('');
          setSeciliModelId('');
          setSeciliVaryantId('');
          setModeller([]);
          setVaryantlar([]);
          setVaryantBakimBilgisi({
            onerilen_bakim_km: null,
            triger_degisim_km: null,
            calisma_saati_bakim: null,
          });
        }
      })
      .catch(() => setMarkalar([]));
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [isModalOpen, seciliKategoriId, formData.marka]);
  // Marka seçildiğinde modelleri yükle
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!seciliMarkaId) {
      setModeller([]);
      setSeciliModelId('');
      setVaryantlar([]);
      setSeciliVaryantId('');
      return;
    }
    let active = true;
    katalogService
      .getModeller(seciliMarkaId, seciliKategoriId || undefined)
      .then((list) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setModeller(list);
        if (formData.model) {
          const eslesenModel = list.find(
            (m) => m.ad.toLowerCase() === String(formData.model).toLowerCase()
          );
          if (eslesenModel) {
            setSeciliModelId(String(eslesenModel.model_id));
          } else {
            setSeciliModelId('');
          }
        }
      })
      .catch(() => setModeller([]));
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [seciliMarkaId, seciliKategoriId, formData.model]);
  // Model seçildiğinde varyantları yükle
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!seciliModelId) {
      setVaryantlar([]);
      setSeciliVaryantId('');
      return;
    }
    let active = true;
    katalogService
      .getVaryantlar(seciliModelId)
      .then((list) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setVaryantlar(list);
      })
      .catch(() => setVaryantlar([]));
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [seciliModelId]);
  const handleInputChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setFormField(name, name === 'plaka' ? formatTurkishPlate(value) : value);
  };
  const handleKategoriSelect = (e) => {
    const kategoriId = e.target.value;
    setSeciliKategoriId(kategoriId);
    setSeciliMarkaId('');
    setSeciliModelId('');
    setSeciliVaryantId('');
    setModeller([]);
    setVaryantlar([]);
    setVaryantBakimBilgisi({
      onerilen_bakim_km: null,
      triger_degisim_km: null,
      calisma_saati_bakim: null,
    });
    const kategoriObj = kategoriler.find((k) => String(k.kategori_id) === String(kategoriId));
    setFormField('kategori_id', kategoriId);
    setFormField('kategori', kategoriObj ? kategoriObj.ad : '');
    setFormField('marka', '');
    setFormField('model', '');
    setFormField('onerilen_bakim_km', null);
    setFormField('triger_degisim_km', null);
    setFormField('calisma_saati_bakim', null);
  };
  const handleMarkaSelect = (e) => {
    const markaId = e.target.value;
    setSeciliMarkaId(markaId);
    setSeciliModelId('');
    setSeciliVaryantId('');
    setVaryantBakimBilgisi({
      onerilen_bakim_km: null,
      triger_degisim_km: null,
      calisma_saati_bakim: null,
    });
    const markaObj = markalar.find((m) => String(m.marka_id) === String(markaId));
    setFormField('marka', markaObj ? markaObj.ad : '');
    setFormField('model', '');
  };
  const handleModelSelect = (e) => {
    const modelId = e.target.value;
    setSeciliModelId(modelId);
    setSeciliVaryantId('');
    setVaryantBakimBilgisi({
      onerilen_bakim_km: null,
      triger_degisim_km: null,
      calisma_saati_bakim: null,
    });
    const modelObj = modeller.find((m) => String(m.model_id) === String(modelId));
    setFormField('model', modelObj ? modelObj.ad : '');
  };
  const handleVaryantSelect = (e) => {
    const varyantId = e.target.value;
    setSeciliVaryantId(varyantId);
    const varyantObj = varyantlar.find((v) => String(v.varyant_id) === String(varyantId));
    if (varyantObj) {
      const bakimKm = varyantObj.onerilen_bakim_km ?? null;
      const trigerKm = varyantObj.triger_degisim_km ?? null;
      const calismaSaati = varyantObj.calisma_saati_bakim ?? null;
      setVaryantBakimBilgisi({
        onerilen_bakim_km: bakimKm,
        triger_degisim_km: trigerKm,
        calisma_saati_bakim: calismaSaati,
      });
      setFormField('onerilen_bakim_km', bakimKm);
      setFormField('triger_degisim_km', trigerKm);
      setFormField('calisma_saati_bakim', calismaSaati);
    } else {
      setVaryantBakimBilgisi({
        onerilen_bakim_km: null,
        triger_degisim_km: null,
        calisma_saati_bakim: null,
      });
      setFormField('onerilen_bakim_km', null);
      setFormField('triger_degisim_km', null);
      setFormField('calisma_saati_bakim', null);
    }
  };
  const handleFormEnterKeyDown = (e) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Enter' || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.currentTarget.requestSubmit();
  };
  const handleAddArac = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    submitArac();
  };
  if (!isModalOpen) return null;
  const selectClassName =
    'flex h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25 focus:outline-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50';
  const inputClassName =
    'h-10 rounded-xl border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/25';
  return (
    <div className={`fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 px-4 backdrop-blur-sm ${isModalVisible ? 'modal-backdrop-enter' : 'modal-backdrop-exit'}`}>
      <div className={`w-full max-w-lg max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white/95 p-6 sm:p-7 text-slate-900 dark:border-white/10 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 dark:text-zinc-100 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl ${isModalVisible ? 'modal-panel-enter' : 'modal-panel-exit'}`}>
        <h3 className="mb-5 text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-zinc-100">{editingAracId ? 'Araç Düzenle' : 'Yeni Araç Ekle'}</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleAddArac} onKeyDown={handleFormEnterKeyDown} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Plaka</Label>
            <Input
              ref={plakaInputRef}
              type="text"
              name="plaka"
              value={formData.plaka}
              onChange={handleInputChange}
              autoFocus
              className={inputClassName}
              placeholder="34 ABC 123"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Kategori</Label>
            <select
              name="kategori_id"
              value={seciliKategoriId}
              onChange={handleKategoriSelect}
              className={selectClassName}
            >
              <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">
                -- Kategori Seçin --
              </option>
              {kategoriler.map((k) => (
                <option key={k.kategori_id} value={k.kategori_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  {k.ad}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Marka</Label>
            <select
              name="marka_id"
              value={seciliMarkaId}
              onChange={handleMarkaSelect}
              disabled={!seciliKategoriId}
              className={selectClassName}
            >
              <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {!seciliKategoriId
                  ? 'Önce kategori seçin'
                  : formData.marka
                  ? `${formData.marka} (Değiştirmek için seçin)`
                  : '-- Marka Seçin --'}
              </option>
              {markalar.map((m) => (
                <option key={m.marka_id} value={m.marka_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  {m.ad}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Model</Label>
            <select
              name="model_id"
              value={seciliModelId}
              onChange={handleModelSelect}
              disabled={!seciliMarkaId}
              className={selectClassName}
            >
              <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {!seciliMarkaId
                  ? 'Önce marka seçin'
                  : formData.model
                  ? `${formData.model} (Değiştirmek için seçin)`
                  : '-- Model Seçin --'}
              </option>
              {modeller.map((mo) => (
                <option key={mo.model_id} value={mo.model_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  {mo.ad}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Kasa / Motor Varyantı</Label>
            <select
              name="varyant_id"
              value={seciliVaryantId}
              onChange={handleVaryantSelect}
              disabled={!seciliModelId}
              className={selectClassName}
            >
              <option value="" className="bg-white text-slate-500 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {!seciliModelId ? 'Önce model seçin' : '-- Kasa / Motor Seçin --'}
              </option>
              {varyantlar.map((v) => (
                <option key={v.varyant_id} value={v.varyant_id} className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  {v.etiket || `${v.kasa_tipi} • ${v.motor_adi} • ${v.sanziman_tipi} (${v.yakit_tipi})`}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            {(varyantBakimBilgisi.onerilen_bakim_km || varyantBakimBilgisi.calisma_saati_bakim) && (
              <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {varyantBakimBilgisi.calisma_saati_bakim ? (
                  <>
                    Çalışma Saati Bakımı:{' '}
                    <strong className="text-primary">{varyantBakimBilgisi.calisma_saati_bakim} Saat</strong>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    Önerilen Bakım:{' '}
                    <strong className="text-primary">{varyantBakimBilgisi.onerilen_bakim_km.toLocaleString('tr-TR')} km</strong>
                    {varyantBakimBilgisi.triger_degisim_km
                      ? ` • Triger: ${varyantBakimBilgisi.triger_degisim_km.toLocaleString('tr-TR')} km`
                      : ' • Elektrikli / Zincirli Sürüş'}
                  </>
                )}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
          <div>
            <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Yıl</Label>
            <Input
              type="number"
              name="yil"
              value={formData.yil}
              onChange={handleInputChange}
              className={inputClassName}
              placeholder="2023"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <div>
              <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Muayene Tarihi</Label>
              <TrussDatePicker
                name="muayene_tarihi"
                value={formData.muayene_tarihi}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                placeholder="Muayene seç"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Kasko Tarihi</Label>
              <TrussDatePicker
                name="kasko_tarihi"
                value={formData.kasko_tarihi}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                placeholder="Kasko seç"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <Label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Sigorta Tarihi</Label>
              <TrussDatePicker
                name="sigorta_tarihi"
                value={formData.sigorta_tarihi}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                placeholder="Sigorta seç"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={closeModal}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 px-4 py-2 text-slate-700 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-slate-900 dark:border-zinc-700/80 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:border-primary/50 dark:hover:bg-primary/15 dark:hover:text-white"
            >
              İptal
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              className="neo-btn rounded-xl bg-primary px-5 py-2 font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-md shadow-primary/20 hover:opacity-95"
            >
              {isSubmitting ? (editingAracId ? 'Güncelleniyor...' : 'Ekleniyor...') : (editingAracId ? 'Güncelle' : 'Kaydet')}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
export default AddVehicleModal;
````

## File: Frontend/src/components/dashboard/DashboardStats.js
````javascript
import React from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { AlertTriangle, Clock, CheckCircle2, FileWarning, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-react'
const STATS = [
  {
    key: 'tehlike',
    label: 'Bakımı Geçen',
    icon: AlertTriangle,
    iconClass: 'text-destructive',
    valueClass: 'text-destructive',
    href: '/vehicles?filtre=tehlike',
  },
  {
    key: 'uyari',
    label: 'Bakımı Yaklaşan',
    icon: Clock,
    iconClass: 'text-orange-400',
    valueClass: 'text-foreground',
    href: '/parcalar',
  },
  {
    key: 'sorunsuz',
    label: 'Sorunsuz Araçlar',
    icon: CheckCircle2,
    iconClass: 'text-emerald-500',
    valueClass: 'text-foreground',
    href: '/vehicles',
  },
  {
    key: 'evrakSorunu',
    label: 'Evrak Sorunu',
    icon: FileWarning,
    iconClass: 'text-orange-500',
    valueClass: 'text-foreground',
    href: '/vehicles?filtre=evrak',
  },
  {
    key: 'gelecekAyTahmin',
    label: 'Tahmini Ay Gideri',
    icon: TrendingUp,
    iconClass: 'text-primary',
    valueClass: 'text-foreground',
    href: '/raporlar',
    isCurrency: true,
  },
]
const DashboardStats = ({ filoOzeti, formatTahminTL }) => {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  return (
    <section className="mb-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-5">
      {STATS.map(({ key, label, icon: Icon, iconClass, valueClass, href, isCurrency }) => {
        const raw = filoOzeti[key] ?? 0
        const display = isCurrency ? formatTahminTL(raw) : raw
        return (
          <div
            key={key}
            onClick={() => navigate(href)}
            className="cursor-pointer hover:scale-[1.02] hover:shadow-md transition-all rounded-xl"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-border bg-card p-3.5 transition-colors hover:border-primary/40">
                <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">{label}</span>
                <Icon className={`size-4 shrink-0 ${iconClass}`} />
                <p className={`ml-2 text-lg font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight ${valueClass}`}>{display}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      })}
    </section>
  )
}
export default DashboardStats
````
